- GIVEN a change switched to a set, WHEN synced, THEN the change is undone before the set
- GIVEN a relation with the player, WHEN synced, THEN the step says so
- GIVEN the pair written the other way round, WHEN keyed, THEN it is the same entry

### HostageBreachPolicyTests.cs

_Council Tier 4 (give_hostage v2): this policy decides whether a battle breaks a hostage accord and, if so, whose fault it was, which in turn decides whether the hostage is imprisoned. What breaks if it is wrong: either a hostage is seized when no breach happened (the player jails a guest for a battle that never involved the pledger), or a real betrayal goes unpunished. DIRECTION is the load-bearing fact: the hostage is forfeit ONLY when the PLEDGER struck; a player-initiated attack is the player's own broken faith and must NOT jail the hostage._

- GIVEN the pledger attacks the player, THEN it is a breach
- GIVEN the player attacks the pledger, THEN it is still a breach
- GIVEN the two factions do not face each other, THEN it is not a breach
- GIVEN the pledger is the attacker, THEN the pledger is at fault
- GIVEN the player is the attacker, THEN the pledger is not at fault

### HouseholdKinPolicyTests.cs

_that a lord can be offered one of the player's retainers, and never one of the player's family._

- GIVEN a daughter in law, WHEN asked, THEN she is family and cannot be given away
- GIVEN extended blood family, WHEN asked, THEN they are family
- GIVEN close family the old rule already protected, WHEN asked, THEN they still are
- GIVEN a companion who joined the clan, WHEN asked, THEN he is not family
- GIVEN a marriage recorded only on the other spouse, WHEN asked, THEN it still counts
- GIVEN the sibling of a married in spouse, WHEN asked, THEN he is not family
- GIVEN people with no parents on record, WHEN asked, THEN they are not made kin by it
- GIVEN no player, WHEN asked, THEN nobody is family

### HushMoneyPolicyTests.cs

_HushMoneyPolicy is the numeric contract for blackmail paid to a lord who holds the player's secret-lover identity (mystery-lover phase 3b-3). Two failures matter. If the clamp were dropped, a hallucinated or coerced demand could bankrupt the player (or a lowballed one let them buy silence for a coin). If the cooldown misjudged recency, a paid-off player would be shaken down again the same visit (cooldown too short) or the lord would hold his peace forever after one payment (too long). These pin the band and the cooldown boundary._

- GIVEN a lowball amount, WHEN clamped, THEN it is raised to the floor
- GIVEN an extravagant amount, WHEN clamped, THEN it is capped at the ceiling
- GIVEN a reasonable amount, WHEN clamped, THEN it passes through
- GIVEN a payment within the cooldown, WHEN checking, THEN it is recently paid
- GIVEN the cooldown has elapsed, WHEN checking, THEN it is no longer recently paid
- GIVEN no payment recorded, WHEN checking, THEN it is not recently paid

### InjectedMemoryRemovalPolicyTests.cs

_A mod takes back a memory it added (philip0105, Vivid World, 10/10/2026: a rumor forgotten or denied in his mod lingered in CR, so the two mods told different stories). If this rule is wrong, a forgotten rumor stays in the character's mouth, or one mod's removal erases another mod's memories._

- GIVEN a memory a mod added, WHEN that mod removes its text, THEN it is taken
- GIVEN a memory another mod added, WHEN a mod removes the same text, THEN it stays
- GIVEN a memory stored before mods were recorded, WHEN its exact text is removed, THEN it is taken
- GIVEN a different text, WHEN removed, THEN nothing is taken

### InterceptionPacingTests.cs

_How often lords ride out to have a word with the player. A player asked for the dial outright, and named both reasons in the same breath: "I like NPCs approaching me to kick off conversations" and "I don't want to be forced into a bunch of conversations" (Fakade, 2026-07-13). One player, two opposite needs. That is a setting._

- GIVEN a player who never opens the menu, WHEN lords ride out, THEN nothing whatsoever changes
- GIVEN a player who wants the road to themselves, WHEN set to Never, THEN no lord ever rides out
- GIVEN any setting that is not Never, WHEN asked, THEN the feature is live
- GIVEN the four settings, WHEN ordered, THEN each one up genuinely means more company
- GIVEN more company is asked for, WHEN the cooldown is read, THEN it moves WITH the chance not against it

### InterceptionPolicyTests.cs

_NPC-initiated interceptions: this policy decides TWO things a lord's own AI cannot judge on its own._

- GIVEN a chase past the timeout, WHEN judging, THEN it is abandoned
- GIVEN both parties ashore, WHEN judging reachability, THEN the meeting may be held
- GIVEN the player is at sea, WHEN judging reachability, THEN no meeting may be staged
- GIVEN the pursuer is at sea, WHEN judging reachability, THEN no meeting may be staged
- GIVEN a hostile realm but the player is elsewhere, WHEN choosing, THEN no patrol
- GIVEN a lord who has heard of a recent deed, WHEN choosing, THEN they ride out to react
- GIVEN a nobody in hostile land, WHEN choosing, THEN no one bothers
- GIVEN a notorious player in hostile land but the realm is at peace, WHEN choosing, THEN no patrol
- GIVEN a notorious player in hostile land, WHEN choosing, THEN a territorial patrol rides out
- GIVEN almost caught even past the timeout, WHEN judging, THEN the pursuer still presses on
- GIVEN another pursuer is already active, WHEN choosing, THEN no second interception starts
- GIVEN both a territorial challenge and recent news, WHEN choosing, THEN the patrol takes priority
- GIVEN nothing applies, WHEN choosing, THEN the lord stays their course
- GIVEN notoriety exactly at the floor, WHEN choosing, THEN it still triggers
- GIVEN null facts, WHEN choosing, THEN it is a safe none
- GIVEN the player clearly faster and well clear, WHEN judging, THEN the hopeless chase is given up
- GIVEN the player faster but almost caught, WHEN judging, THEN the pursuer keeps pressing
- GIVEN the player only marginally faster, WHEN judging, THEN the chase continues
- GIVEN the pursuer at least as fast as the player, WHEN judging, THEN they press on
- GIVEN the gap is closing even past the outrun ratio, WHEN judging, THEN the chase is not abandoned
- GIVEN the gap is closing even past the timeout, WHEN judging, THEN the chase is not abandoned
- GIVEN isClosing is not specified, WHEN judging a stale chase, THEN the default still abandons
- GIVEN this lord is on cooldown, WHEN choosing, THEN nothing fires however strong the trigger
- GIVEN a lord who holds a live grudge against the player, WHEN choosing, THEN they come to air it
- GIVEN a lord who holds a significant appreciation of the player, WHEN choosing, THEN they come to acknowledge it
- GIVEN both a territorial challenge and a live grudge, WHEN choosing, THEN the patrol still takes priority
- GIVEN both a live grudge and recent news, WHEN choosing, THEN the grudge takes priority
- GIVEN both recent news and a significant appreciation, WHEN choosing, THEN the news takes priority
- GIVEN every trigger fact at once, WHEN choosing, THEN the full priority order holds
- GIVEN a live grudge but the lord is on cooldown, WHEN choosing, THEN nothing fires
- GIVEN a significant appreciation but another pursuer is active, WHEN choosing, THEN nothing fires
- GIVEN a lord with an eligible nemesis bounty to offer, WHEN choosing, THEN they come to announce it
- GIVEN both a territorial challenge and a nemesis bounty, WHEN choosing, THEN the patrol still takes priority
- GIVEN both a live grudge and a nemesis bounty, WHEN choosing, THEN the grudge takes priority
- GIVEN both a nemesis bounty and recent news, WHEN choosing, THEN the nemesis bounty takes priority
- GIVEN both a nemesis bounty and a significant appreciation, WHEN choosing, THEN the nemesis bounty takes priority
- GIVEN an eligible nemesis bounty but the lord is on cooldown, WHEN choosing, THEN nothing fires
- GIVEN an eligible nemesis bounty but another pursuer is active, WHEN choosing, THEN nothing fires
- GIVEN a chase abandoned today, WHEN judging the grace window, THEN the intent is active
- GIVEN a chase abandoned within the window, WHEN judging, THEN the intent is active
- GIVEN a chase abandoned past the window, WHEN judging, THEN the intent is gone
- GIVEN no remembered intent, WHEN judging, THEN it is never active
- GIVEN an ordinary give up, WHEN judging the letter, THEN the lord writes
- GIVEN a living pursuer drawn into other business, WHEN judging the letter, THEN he writes
- GIVEN a dead pursuer, WHEN judging the letter, THEN no letter is written
- GIVEN a chase lost at sea, WHEN judging the letter, THEN no letter is written

### IntimacyCurrencyPolicyTests.cs

_Protects the intimacy-as-currency eligibility gate (Negotiation Phase 3) — strict, one-directional, archetype-filtered, adult-gated. No content here, only who may raise it._

- GIVEN a cynical unrelated man toward a female player at an explicit tier, WHEN asked, THEN eligible
- GIVEN a male player, WHEN asked, THEN not eligible
- GIVEN a woman npc, WHEN asked, THEN not eligible
- GIVEN the npc is the players kin, WHEN asked, THEN not eligible
- GIVEN a tier below explicit, WHEN asked, THEN not eligible
- GIVEN an honourable man, WHEN asked, THEN not eligible
- GIVEN a dishonourable but neither scheming nor callous man, WHEN asked, THEN not eligible
- GIVEN a dishonourable callous man, WHEN asked, THEN eligible even without scheming

### IntimacyPacingRulesTests.cs

_Adult-prompt audit M3. This maps the player's Relationship Pacing dial onto the intimacy consent bar, which until now was the one gate in the mod that ignored the dial entirely. The answer feeds BOTH the verdict printed in the prompt and the mechanical gate in the bridge, so a wrong value here does not just reword a paragraph: it changes how hard intimacy is in a live campaign. The Grounded zero matters most of all, because it is what keeps the whole feature invisible for a player who never touches the setting._

- GIVEN the grounded dial, WHEN asking the relief, THEN the bar is left exactly where it shipped
- GIVEN the warmer dials, WHEN asking the relief, THEN each step lowers the bar further
- GIVEN any dial setting, WHEN asking the relief, THEN it never raises the bar
- GIVEN an unknown dial value, WHEN asking the relief, THEN it falls back to the balanced level

### IntimateChroniclePolicyTests.cs

_Documents the Chronicle's notable-intimate-conquest gate: nothing at all with adult content off or a nobody partner, tasteful period prose at Mature/Explicit, bawdier (still never explicit) at Hardcore._

- GIVEN adult content is off, WHEN building a captive conquest line, THEN nothing is recorded
- GIVEN the partner is a nobody, WHEN building a captive conquest line, THEN nothing is recorded
- GIVEN a missing name, WHEN building a line, THEN nothing is recorded
- GIVEN mature content AND a notable captive, WHEN building the line, THEN it reads tasteful
- GIVEN hardcore content AND a notable captive, WHEN building the line, THEN it reads bawdier
- GIVEN a notable male captive, WHEN building the line, THEN the honorific is lord
- GIVEN the partner is the mother, WHEN building a conception line, THEN her name leads the begetting
- GIVEN the player is the mother, WHEN building a conception line, THEN her name leads the begetting
- GIVEN hardcore content, WHEN building a conception line, THEN it reads bawdier but never explicit

### JealousyCultureTests.cs

_Protects the heart of the jealousy design — whether a culture reads a new partner as BETRAYAL or merely COMPETITION — and the raw-value parsing that decides it. A silent regression here would turn every faction monogamous (or every faction permissive), unnoticed in game._

- GIVEN a monogamous culture, WHEN asked at any tier, THEN it never tolerates sharing
- GIVEN a permissive culture at or above the elite tier, WHEN asked, THEN it tolerates sharing
- GIVEN a permissive culture below the elite tier, WHEN asked, THEN it behaves monogamously
- GIVEN a high outrage culture above the tier gate, WHEN asked, THEN it still refuses to share
- GIVEN outrage strings in any casing, WHEN parsed, THEN they map to the right level
- GIVEN an unrecognised or null outrage, WHEN parsed, THEN it degrades to monogamous
- GIVEN hierarchy strings, WHEN parsed, THEN only high and medium are recognised
- GIVEN a null prompt hint, WHEN built, THEN it is an empty string

### JealousyHearingPolicyTests.cs

_Guards JealousyHearingPolicy.WithinEarshot, the earshot gate JealousyBehavior.EvaluateAwareness adds ON TOP of WorldEventStore.IsAwareOf. Player report: the spouse seemed "psychic", learning of a liaison instantly from the far end of the map because the shared rumour scoring's same-faction bonus ignores distance. This gate pins the jealousy-specific truth: word must have reached their circles AND they must be close enough to where it happened, or be riding beside the player, in which case the affair happens under their nose and no distance argument applies._

- GIVEN a party beside the player, WHEN gating hearing, THEN they always hear
- GIVEN a party within earshot, WHEN gating hearing, THEN they hear
- GIVEN a party beyond earshot, WHEN gating hearing, THEN they have not heard
- GIVEN an unknown distance, WHEN gating hearing, THEN they have not heard

### JealousyReactionPolicyTests.cs

_Jealousy, witnessed-reaction gate (romance audit M-J1/M-J2). This is the single decision "does a witnessed romantic act draw a jealous reaction this time". It guards two real bugs: a MARRIAGE-tier act (a public wedding, a consort openly taken) must ALWAYS wake the jealous party, never be swallowed by the 1-in-4 travel roll (M-J2); and because the host consumes the pairing's 20-day cooldown only when this returns true, a failed lesser-act roll must return false so it never burns the cooldown and swallows a real marriage days later (M-J1). Both are silent betrayals of the fiction ("your spouse was taken and you never heard"), so the boundaries are pinned._

- GIVEN a marriage tier act, WHEN the roll would fail, THEN it still reacts
- GIVEN a lesser act, WHEN the roll lands within the chance, THEN it reacts
- GIVEN a lesser act, WHEN the roll exceeds the chance, THEN it does not react
- GIVEN a lesser act, WHEN the roll equals the chance, THEN it reacts
- GIVEN a private marriage tier act, WHEN the roll would fail, THEN it still reacts
- GIVEN a private flirt, WHEN the roll lands within the chance, THEN it reacts
- GIVEN a private flirt, WHEN the roll exceeds the chance, THEN it does not react
- GIVEN a private flirt, WHEN the roll equals the chance, THEN it does not react

### JealousyReactionTests.cs

_Protects the jealousy reaction barèmes: the relation hit, the letter likelihood, and the betrayal-vs-competition framing. These numbers and phrasings shape how the mechanic FEELS and regress in silence — a wrong scale or a swapped branch never throws._

- GIVEN a betrayed exclusive partner, WHEN scored, THEN the hit grows with the act
- GIVEN a sharing culture partner, WHEN scored, THEN competition stings far less than betrayal
- GIVEN a competition hit, WHEN scored, THEN culture outrage does not scale it
- GIVEN a courted NPCs spouse, WHEN outrage is lower, THEN the hit scales down but never to zero
- GIVEN a rival or spurned admirer, WHEN scored at high outrage, THEN it uses the base scale
- GIVEN each stake, WHEN asked for the letter chance, THEN the rival is likeliest to write
- GIVEN a player partner, WHEN the grievance is built, THEN betrayal and competition read differently
- GIVEN a culture hint, WHEN a letter context is built, THEN it carries the names and the hint
- GIVEN no writer name, WHEN a letter context is built, THEN it falls back to a safe label
- GIVEN a secret lover, WHEN scored, THEN the hurt is real but milder than open betrayal
- GIVEN a secret lover, WHEN scored, THEN culture outrage never scales the private wound
- GIVEN a secret lover, WHEN the letter context is built, THEN it stays private and never threatens exposure
- GIVEN a secret lover, WHEN the grievance is built, THEN it reads as quiet hurt not menace

### JealousyTriggerPolicyTests.cs

_Guards JealousyTriggerPolicy.IsPlausibleCourtship, the gate ChatViewModel.Exchanges consults before ever calling JealousyBehavior.OnRomanticAct. Player report (v1.29.0): a male player did a male lord a service and the lord's WIFE sent jealous threats over a pair that could never be a courtship._

- GIVEN an implausible pair, WHEN evaluating courtship, THEN no jealousy ever fires
- GIVEN an orientation sanctioned same sex pair, WHEN evaluating, THEN it counts like any courtship
- GIVEN a first ever flirt, WHEN evaluating courtship, THEN no jealousy fires
- GIVEN a repeated flirtation pattern, WHEN evaluating, THEN jealousy may fire
- GIVEN only two flirts, WHEN evaluating, THEN no jealousy fires yet
- GIVEN a flirt stamped on a quest turn in, WHEN evaluating, THEN it is read as gratitude never courtship
- GIVEN an intimacy deed, WHEN evaluating, THEN it always counts even on a first event
- GIVEN an intimacy deed on a quest turn in, WHEN evaluating, THEN it still counts

### KeepPeaceBreachPolicyTests.cs

_Guards the v1.34.1 hotfix for swear_oath's "keep the peace" kind. The shipped v1.34.0 breach check marked the oath broken whenever the promisor's faction and the named house ended up at war, without ever reading WHO DECLARED it, so a lord who swore peace and was then INVADED was branded an oathbreaker for the aggression committed against him. KeepPeaceBreachPolicy decides the breach purely: a keep-peace oath breaks ONLY when the promisor's own faction was the aggressor (the declarer). If this rule drifts, the exploit returns (a victim of invasion loses relation, eats a BrokenOath grudge and a per-witness cost for a war they never chose) or, the opposite failure, a genuine aggressor escapes their broken oath entirely._

- GIVEN the promisor declared war on the oath house, WHEN judging, THEN it breaches
- GIVEN the oath house invaded the promisor, WHEN judging, THEN it does NOT breach
- GIVEN the promisor declared war on a different house, WHEN judging, THEN it does NOT breach
- GIVEN a war between two unrelated houses, WHEN judging, THEN it does NOT breach
- GIVEN case differences, WHEN judging, THEN matching is case insensitive
- GIVEN a missing id, WHEN judging, THEN it does NOT breach

### KeywordTokenTests.cs

_Documents + protects the scripted-scenarios keyword language: VERB / VERB:payload, the first-colon split, case + whitespace normalization, and the wildcard. The modder writes these strings, so the parse contract must stay exactly as shown here._

- GIVEN a bare verb, WHEN parsing, THEN the payload is empty and it is a wildcard
- GIVEN a verb with one argument, WHEN parsing, THEN verb and payload split on the colon
- GIVEN a comma payload, WHEN parsing, THEN args split on commas and trim
- GIVEN a payload with further colons, WHEN parsing, THEN only the first colon splits
- GIVEN lowercase and padding, WHEN parsing, THEN the verb is uppercased and trimmed
- GIVEN a lone star payload, WHEN parsing, THEN it is a wildcard
- GIVEN blank input, WHEN parsing, THEN it yields an empty verb token without throwing
- GIVEN two tokens differing only in case, WHEN compared, THEN they are equal

### KinDeathDescriberTests.cs

_Pins how a dead relative is worded in an NPC's RELATIONSHIPS block. Born from Maifailun's report (through tashmetu, 15/10/2026): the player's wife Debana died in childbirth and her father asked "Which Debana?" about his own daughter._

- GIVEN a daughter wed to the player who died in childbirth, WHEN worded, THEN both the marriage and the death are said
- GIVEN a son fallen in battle, WHEN worded, THEN it reads how and when
- GIVEN a death today, WHEN worded, THEN it reads this very day
- GIVEN a death years ago, WHEN worded, THEN it is counted in years
- GIVEN no usable death day, WHEN worded, THEN no time is invented
- GIVEN the players late husband with no death day, WHEN worded, THEN the marriage is still said
- GIVEN an unworded death mark, WHEN worded, THEN it reads plainly died
- GIVEN an execution after a battle, WHEN worded, THEN it reads executed

### KinkParserTests.cs

_Guards the mapping from author-written kink names (character_overrides.json "kinks" field) to Kink values. Player request: kinks added with cr.add_kink were wiped on cr.reload_lore (RederiveKinks re-derives from current traits every reload); authoring them in the override file instead locks them so they survive a reload and let the player stage a scenario. The parsed list feeds CharacterOverride.ParsedKinks, which CharacterLoreBehavior and the per-turn chat sync both apply onto NpcProfile.Romantic.Kinks, REPLACING whatever was rolled/frozen. A name that fails to parse must be skipped quietly, never throw: a typo in a hand-edited file must never crash the campaign._

- GIVEN a known kink name, WHEN parsing, THEN it maps to that kink
- GIVEN a mix of valid and unknown names, WHEN parsing, THEN only the valid ones survive
- GIVEN a duplicated name, WHEN parsing, THEN only the first occurrence is kept
- GIVEN blank or whitespace entries, WHEN parsing, THEN they are ignored
- GIVEN null names, WHEN parsing, THEN it returns an empty list
- GIVEN an empty collection, WHEN parsing, THEN it returns an empty list
- GIVEN typos among valid names, WHEN listing unrecognized, THEN only the typos are reported
- GIVEN only valid names, WHEN listing unrecognized, THEN none are reported
- GIVEN blank or null input, WHEN listing unrecognized, THEN nothing is reported

### LetterAgeDescriberTests.cs

_Player report: a letter written before current events read as a fresh, incoherent message with no sense of when it was actually sent. This decides the age line LetterViewModel shows in the read window's heading ("From X, sent 3 days ago"), so a dispatch reads as the dated note it is. Get the boundary wrong and either a same-day letter falsely claims to be old, or a letter is silently dated into the future, both of which would misrepresent when the sender actually wrote it._

- GIVEN a letter written today, WHEN describing its age, THEN it reads sent today
- GIVEN a letter written yesterday, WHEN describing its age, THEN it reads sent yesterday
- GIVEN a letter several days old, WHEN describing its age, THEN it counts the days
- GIVEN an unknown writing day, WHEN describing, THEN nothing is said
- GIVEN a writing day in the future, WHEN describing, THEN nothing is said

### LetterArchivePolicyTests.cs

_The correspondence history is unbounded by design (2026-08-24): losing an old letter has no value to the player, so nothing is ever evicted (the eviction machinery was removed). This policy is now only membership: whether a letter counts as archived correspondence (delivered, not lost, with a body) and whether a contact has any correspondence at all. That last answer shows or hides the archive entry on the hero's encyclopedia page and the in-letter correspondence panel: a false positive would offer an empty archive; a false negative would hide a real history the player wrote._

- GIVEN a delivered letter in either direction, WHEN asked, THEN correspondence exists
- GIVEN only an in transit letter, WHEN asked, THEN no correspondence exists

### LetterCadenceTests.cs

_This is the anti-spam half of the letter system, shared by every unprompted letter type (romantic, spouse, consort, marriage solicitation, battle/tournament congratulations). LetterTriggerEvaluator's daily tick calls straight into these gates to decide whether a letter may queue today. Get it too permissive and the player is buried in mail from every correspondent at once (the tester-reported failure GlobalLetterCooldownDays exists to stop); get it too restrictive and the world goes mute, breaking the "the world remembers you" feel._

- GIVEN a letter still in transit, WHEN checking, THEN no new letter may be sent
- GIVEN an undelivered but LOST letter, WHEN checking, THEN a new letter may still be sent
- GIVEN a null profile, WHEN checking, THEN a letter is treated as sendable
- GIVEN an npc who has never written, WHEN checking, THEN the first letter is allowed
- GIVEN no recorded events, WHEN measuring time since last, THEN the npc is maximally overdue
- GIVEN no unprompted letter has ever been sent, WHEN checking globally, THEN one is allowed
- GIVEN one was sent within the global window, WHEN checking, THEN the world stays quiet
- GIVEN recorded events, WHEN measuring time since last, THEN it counts from the most recent
- GIVEN several past letters, WHEN checking, THEN the cooldown runs from the most recent
- GIVEN the cooldown has elapsed since the last letter, WHEN checking, THEN a new one is allowed
- GIVEN the cooldown lands exactly on today, WHEN checking, THEN it is allowed
- GIVEN the global window has elapsed, WHEN checking, THEN correspondence may resume
- GIVEN the last letter arrived but the cooldown has not elapsed, WHEN checking, THEN it is blocked

### LetterCandidateFreshnessTests.cs

_This policy decides whether a queued LetterCandidateIntent still deserves to compete for the mailbox, or should be quietly retired instead. It feeds LetterTriggerEvaluator.CollectEventCandidates, which drops a stale intent from NpcProfile.LetterCandidates before it ever reaches the pool. We do NOT need to deliver every letter, only the ones that still matter: a queued congratulation outcompeted for weeks is worse to deliver late than to retire quietly, so if this policy calls something fresh when it should not, stale news leaks to the player; if it calls something stale too early, a candidate that still mattered is lost forever._

- GIVEN a candidate enqueued today, WHEN checked, THEN it is fresh
- GIVEN a candidate exactly at its freshness window edge, WHEN checked, THEN it is still fresh
- GIVEN a candidate one day past its freshness window, WHEN checked, THEN it is stale
- GIVEN a congratulation reason, WHEN the window is read, THEN it is fifteen days
- GIVEN a twenty day old birth announcement, WHEN checked, THEN it is still fresh
- GIVEN a twenty day old battle victory, WHEN checked, THEN it is already stale
- GIVEN a reason with no dedicated window, WHEN the window is read, THEN it falls back to the default
- GIVEN a battle congratulation from a realm the player has left, WHEN it comes up, THEN it no longer holds
- GIVEN a letter that never depended on the realm, WHEN the player changes realm, THEN it still holds

### LetterComposerStateTests.cs

_Guards the courier window's mode rules: a blank reply or a letter the player can't pay for must never seal, and each mode must show exactly the right parchment. A drift here loses words or gold silently._

- GIVEN reading a letter, WHEN judging send, THEN it is never sendable
- GIVEN a blank draft, WHEN judging send, THEN it cannot send
- GIVEN a composed letter the player cannot pay for, WHEN judging send, THEN it cannot send
- GIVEN a composed letter the player can afford, WHEN judging send, THEN it can send
- GIVEN a reply, WHEN judging send, THEN no courier fee applies
- GIVEN a negative courier cost, WHEN constructed, THEN it is clamped to free
- GIVEN read mode, WHEN laying out, THEN only the received body and choices show
- GIVEN compose mode, WHEN laying out, THEN only the writing area and send show
- GIVEN reply mode, WHEN laying out, THEN both the received body and the writing area show

### LetterDeliveryPolicyTests.cs

_Documents the two delivery concerns kept separate: divorce letters always reach the player regardless of proximity, while loneliness/longing letters defer only while their sender stands beside the player._

- GIVEN the spouse divorce demand reason, WHEN checking guaranteed delivery, THEN it is true
- GIVEN the spouse divorce escalation reason, WHEN checking guaranteed delivery, THEN it is true
- GIVEN a romantic correspondence reason, WHEN checking guaranteed delivery, THEN it is false
- GIVEN a spouse correspondence reason, WHEN checking guaranteed delivery, THEN it is false
- GIVEN a birth announcement reason, WHEN checking guaranteed delivery, THEN it is false
- GIVEN a romantic correspondence reason, WHEN checking defer when near, THEN it is true
- GIVEN a spouse correspondence reason, WHEN checking defer when near, THEN it is true
- GIVEN an awaiting reply reason, WHEN checking defer when near, THEN it is true
- GIVEN a spouse divorce demand reason, WHEN checking defer when near, THEN it is false
- GIVEN a tournament victory reason, WHEN checking defer when near, THEN it is false
- GIVEN the player is at sea, WHEN checking the sea gate, THEN delivery is held back
- GIVEN the player is ashore, WHEN checking the sea gate, THEN delivery is not held back
- GIVEN a birth announcement reason, WHEN checking defer when near, THEN it is false
- GIVEN the sender is near, WHEN the reason is romantic correspondence, THEN delivery is deferred
- GIVEN the sender is near, WHEN the reason is spouse divorce demand, THEN delivery is not deferred
- GIVEN the sender is far, WHEN the reason is romantic correspondence, THEN delivery is not deferred
- GIVEN the sender is near, WHEN the reason is birth announcement, THEN delivery is not deferred
- GIVEN a reason whose intention can lapse, WHEN checking intention bearing, THEN it is true
- GIVEN a factual or proximity only reason, WHEN checking intention bearing, THEN it is false
- GIVEN a divorce demand whose intention has lapsed, WHEN checking stale, THEN it is dropped
- GIVEN a war threat whose sender made peace, WHEN checking stale, THEN it is dropped
- GIVEN a divorce demand whose intention still holds, WHEN checking stale, THEN it is kept
- GIVEN a factual announcement, WHEN its intention is reported gone, THEN it is never dropped as stale

### LetterDistancePolicyTests.cs

_Documents that a spouse's absence letter is a graduated chance driven by distance in travel days: silent while the player is near, rising only past halfway across the map, capped so it is never a certainty._

- GIVEN the player is near, WHEN computing the chance, THEN no absence letter
- GIVEN the player is at the far edge, WHEN computing the chance, THEN the ceiling but never certain
- GIVEN the player is halfway across the band, WHEN computing the chance, THEN about half the ceiling
- GIVEN the player is just past near, WHEN computing the chance, THEN a small but nonzero chance
- GIVEN the chance rises monotonically, WHEN moving farther, THEN it never decreases

### LetterFontPolicyTests.cs

_Decides whether the parchment letter view swaps the decorative Galahad font for a plain Cyrillic-safe one (CR.Letter.Body/Heading/Input.Plain sibling brushes in GUI/Brushes/CalradiaRemembersLetter.xml, picked by LetterViewModel's BodyBrush/HeadingBrush/InputBrush bindings). Wrong here means either Russian keeps rendering Galahad's broken per-glyph fallback (the player report this policy exists to fix), or a Latin language loses its decorative parchment look for no reason._

- GIVEN the Russian native display name, WHEN deciding, THEN it prefers the plain font
- GIVEN the English word Russian, WHEN deciding, THEN it prefers the plain font
- GIVEN English, WHEN deciding, THEN it keeps the decorative font
- GIVEN French, WHEN deciding, THEN it keeps the decorative font
- GIVEN a differently cased and padded Russian id, WHEN deciding, THEN it still matches
- GIVEN null or empty or an unknown language id, WHEN deciding, THEN it keeps the decorative font

### LetterIntentionPolicyTests.cs

_Documents the per-reason "does the letter's intention still hold at delivery?" rules. A letter must never outlive the intent that wrote it: a reconciled spouse's divorce letter, a lord's war letter after peace, a marriage proposal to a now-married player, and so on, are dropped. The live world is a stub here so each rule reads as a plain statement of the signal it depends on._

- GIVEN a divorce demand, WHEN the spouse no longer demands it, THEN the intention does not hold
- GIVEN a divorce escalation, WHEN the spouse is no longer estranging, THEN the intention does not hold
- GIVEN a reinforcement request, WHEN the sender made peace, THEN the intention does not hold
- GIVEN an open enmity letter, WHEN the sender is no longer hostile, THEN the intention does not hold
- GIVEN a friendship letter, WHEN the sender soured, THEN the intention does not hold
- GIVEN a marriage proposal, WHEN the player is now married, THEN the intention does not hold
- GIVEN a jealous threat, WHEN no live jealousy grudge remains, THEN the intention does not hold
- GIVEN a corruption attempt, WHEN the sender is no longer adverse, THEN the intention does not hold
- GIVEN a blackmail, WHEN either the spouse or the secret lover is gone, THEN the intention does not hold
- GIVEN a reason that carries no lapsing intent, WHEN asked, THEN it always holds
- GIVEN a null world, WHEN asked, THEN it holds so nothing is swallowed
- GIVEN a war summons in transit, WHEN the reckoning closed before it arrived, THEN it is stale

### LetterLeakConsequencePolicyTests.cs

_Intercepted-letter-leak Inc 2: once a leak fires, these are the two magnitudes its lasting consequences use, the grudge the antagonist forms and the renown the player loses as their words become known. This is the ONLY place those numbers are pinned, and the courier code that applies them (SecretReactionPolicy, for a letter read against the reader's interests) trusts this policy for both. If the scaling is wrong, a bitter enemy who reads your secret costs no more than a stranger's mild hostility (the leak has no teeth), or a rare, default-off event turns punishing out of proportion._

- GIVEN an antagonist who already held a grudge, WHEN scaling the consequences, THEN both are harsher than a merely hostile lord
- GIVEN the antagonist kind, WHEN scaling, THEN the pinned values are returned
- GIVEN either antagonist kind, WHEN costing renown, THEN the hit stays small

### LetterLeakPolicyTests.cs

_What makes an intercepted letter anyone's secret: substance. Since 26/09/2026 the old leak roll and antagonist pick are gone (Gabriel: an intercepted letter with substance ALWAYS becomes a secret carried in the world, see CourierSecretPolicyTests); what remains is the bar a letter must clear, so a one-line note is never a "secret"._

- GIVEN content length, THEN substance tracks the minimum

### LetterMailboxPolicyTests.cs

_This is the mailbox scheduler itself: the last gate between "an NPC has a reason to write" and "a letter actually reaches the player." It feeds the delivery step directly (whatever SelectNext returns is what gets sent); everything upstream (LetterWeights, LetterPacingRules) only matters through this policy. Get the budget check wrong and a 100h player with dozens of correspondents drowns in mail; get the ordering or aging wrong and the loudest subject never yields the floor, so a patient low-priority letter (or the player's own reply-worthy correspondent) waits forever; get diversity wrong and the mailbox becomes a monotone feed of whichever reason always scores highest._

- GIVEN the window has already spent its quota, WHEN selecting, THEN nothing is released
- GIVEN no candidates are eligible, WHEN selecting, THEN nothing is released
- GIVEN a high weight and a low weight candidate, WHEN the diversity roll misses, THEN the higher weight wins
- GIVEN a low weight candidate has waited long enough, WHEN selecting, THEN aging lets it overtake a fresher higher weight candidate
- GIVEN the diversity roll hits, WHEN a strictly lower weight candidate exists, THEN it is released instead of the top candidate
- GIVEN every candidate shares the same weight, WHEN the diversity roll hits, THEN there is nothing lower to divert to so the top still wins
- GIVEN a guaranteed reason, WHEN asked, THEN it bypasses the budget
- GIVEN an ordinary flavor reason, WHEN asked, THEN it does NOT bypass the budget
- GIVEN a lost letter, WHEN checking budget membership, THEN it does NOT count
- GIVEN a guaranteed bypass reason, WHEN checking budget membership, THEN it does NOT count
- GIVEN a delivered budgeted letter, WHEN checking budget membership, THEN it counts
- GIVEN identical inputs, WHEN selecting twice, THEN the same candidate is chosen both times

### LetterPacingTests.cs

_This policy decides the player-facing pacing dial for unprompted letters: how many are allowed to reach them per rolling window, and how often a lower-weight one is injected for diversity. It feeds the LetterBudget that LetterMailboxPolicy.SelectNext enforces. Modelled on InterceptionPacing: get the default wrong and every existing player's mailbox behaviour changes the moment this ships, even though nobody asked for anything to change; get a non-default setting wrong and the dial the player reached for does not actually do anything._

- GIVEN a player who never opens the menu, WHEN the mailbox budget is read, THEN it matches the shipped defaults exactly
- GIVEN the three settings, WHEN ordered, THEN each one up genuinely means a fuller mailbox
- GIVEN the three settings, WHEN diversity chance is read, THEN it rises from Rarely to Often

### LetterRegardPolicyTests.cs

_Regard earned by letter. Lordfadooboo (Nexus, 28/09/2026): "seems off to be exchanging letters with someone a dozen times and yet when you meet them again they are still only a +1 or something like that. perhaps you wouldnt get as much through letters though which would make complete sense." Ruled by Gabriel the same day: a warm letter earns +1 at most once every 5 days per correspondent, correspondence alone never lifts regard past "friendly regard", and a cold or insulting letter costs 1. The character judges the letter in the call that already decides the reply; the mod decides what it is worth. If this is wrong, either letters stay worthless (the report), or a player farms regard by writing a letter a day and never needs to meet anyone, which is what the chat's own gate exists to prevent._

- GIVEN a warm letter, WHEN it is read, THEN the writer is thought of a little better
- GIVEN a warm letter soon after the last one that counted, WHEN read, THEN it earns nothing more
- GIVEN the warmth setting, WHEN the wait between two letter gains is asked, THEN warmer settings wait less
- GIVEN a correspondent already at friendly regard, WHEN a warm letter is read, THEN it earns nothing
- GIVEN a cold letter, WHEN read, THEN it costs a point even right after another
- GIVEN a correspondent already wary, WHEN a cold letter is read, THEN it costs nothing more
- GIVEN a letter judged neither warm nor cold, WHEN read, THEN nothing changes
- GIVEN a reply, WHEN its tone line is read, THEN the judgement is found
- GIVEN the prompts that ask for the judgement, WHEN built, THEN they ask for the line this parser reads
- GIVEN a reply carrying its tone line, WHEN the letter is kept, THEN the line is gone

### LetterReplyDecisionParserTests.cs

_This parser turns an LLM's terse control line into the decision "does this NPC answer the player's letter, and when". It is the ONLY thing standing between a weak model's loosely-formatted output and a dispatched courier, so each branch here maps to a concrete failure the parser must prevent: a reply mis-read as a pass leaves the player's letter forever unanswered; a pass mis-read as a reply dispatches a blank courier; an out-of-range delay parks (or races) the courier on a nonsense schedule. The overriding rule the tests pin is TOLERANCE: anything that is not unmistakably a pass must still reach the player as a letter, because a model that ignored the format should never cost the player a reply._

- GIVEN a PASS line, WHEN parsing, THEN the npc does not reply
- GIVEN a bare PASS with no reason, WHEN parsing, THEN it still passes with an empty reason
- GIVEN a plain language refusal, WHEN parsing, THEN it passes
- GIVEN a bare letter body, WHEN parsing, THEN it is a reply on the default delay
- GIVEN a DELAY line then a body, WHEN parsing, THEN the delay is read and stripped from the body
- GIVEN an out of range or garbled delay, WHEN parsing, THEN it uses the default delay
- GIVEN a DELAY line with no body, WHEN parsing, THEN it is a reply with an empty body
- GIVEN a delay exactly on a boundary, WHEN parsing, THEN it is accepted
- GIVEN empty or null input, WHEN parsing, THEN it passes without throwing

### LetterReplyReasonTests.cs

_What a character's reply to the player's letter is about. IdentityCrysys (Nexus, 08/10/2026): "I think I just acknowledged the first letter and replied to every other ... she keeps mentioning the player's silence in every letter", with memories that read "Player wrote to me, and I replied (to ask why no reply had come)" day after day. A reply took the occasion of the letter it answered, so once the player answered her "why no reply?" follow-up, every reply after it was another "why no reply?": the player had written, and she wrote of his silence. A reply to that follow-up goes on with what the thread is about; a follow-up is only ever sent for a romantic letter left unanswered._

- GIVEN the player answered a why no reply letter, WHEN she replies, THEN her reply is about their correspondence
- GIVEN any other letter answered, WHEN replied to, THEN the occasion carries on

### LetterThreadAssemblerTests.cs

_The letter archive is a player-requested feature: read your whole correspondence with a contact as a dated, threaded conversation. This assembler is that thread's only source of truth (the in-game viewer just binds it), so these tests pin what actually reaches the player: entries oldest-first, the exact header wording per direction ("Letter from X" vs "Your letter/reply to X") dated in the Calradian calendar, a player's inline reply surfaced as its own entry, and in-flight/lost/blank letters kept out._

- GIVEN an incoming letter, WHEN building its header, THEN it reads letter from contact with the date
- GIVEN a letter written in a lords name, WHEN the thread is built, THEN it is in the lords history under the title
- GIVEN an outgoing letter, WHEN building its header, THEN it reads your letter to contact
- GIVEN an outgoing reply, WHEN building its header, THEN it reads your reply to contact
- GIVEN an unknown day, WHEN building a header, THEN the date clause is omitted
- GIVEN letters in both directions, WHEN assembling the thread, THEN it is ordered oldest first
- GIVEN a received letter with an inline reply, WHEN assembling, THEN the reply is its own outgoing entry
- GIVEN undelivered or blank letters, WHEN assembling, THEN they are excluded
- GIVEN far more than thirty letters, WHEN assembling with no cap, THEN every one is kept
- GIVEN more letters than the cap, WHEN assembling, THEN only the newest are kept

### LetterWeightsTests.cs

_This policy decides the base priority weight of a letter's subject before the mailbox scheduler ever sees budget or aging: it feeds LetterMailboxPolicy.SelectNext (via LetterCandidate.Weight) and, transitively, every choice about which unprompted letter reaches a 100h player who has dozens of correspondents at once._

- GIVEN every LetterReason the SDK defines, WHEN weighed, THEN each one is explicitly mapped to its pinned tier
- GIVEN a LetterReason nobody has mapped yet, WHEN weighed, THEN it degrades to the low default instead of throwing
- GIVEN an intrigue letter and a flavor letter, WHEN weighed, THEN intrigue outranks flavor
- GIVEN a disadvantage ratio, WHEN the battle victory weight is computed, THEN it matches the agreed palier
- GIVEN ratios from even upward, WHEN compared, THEN the weight never decreases
- GIVEN a lopsided stomp, WHEN weighed, THEN it scores below ordinary flavor not merely at the base
- GIVEN a guaranteed bypass reason, WHEN weighed, THEN it carries the sentinel above every other tier
- GIVEN dampers that would push below the floor, WHEN combined with a base weight, THEN the result floors at ten
- GIVEN a negative interest damper, WHEN applied, THEN the weight drops below the base
- GIVEN an interest value above zero, WHEN applied, THEN it still simply adds no extra clamping here
- GIVEN all interests are neutral, WHEN applied, THEN the base weight passes through unchanged

### LetterWhereaboutsPolicyTests.cs

_Where a letter says its writer and the player are. Lordfadooboo (Nexus, 27/09/2026, on 2.6.5): a wanderer he had beaten in a tournament wrote of him "riding in the field with THEIR retinue", though he rode alone and a wanderer has no soldiers. The writer's phrase is addressed to the writer ("with your own retinue") and was reused for the player, so the letter told the writer that the player marched with the writer's own retinue. If the player's phrase ever borrows that "your" again, the same letter comes back; if it invents a company, a lone player is given one._

- GIVEN the player riding alone, WHEN the letter places them, THEN they ride alone and nobody elses retinue is named
- GIVEN the player with a company, WHEN placed, THEN it is their own company
- GIVEN someone in a town, WHEN placed, THEN the town is named for either
- GIVEN the writer on the march, WHEN placed, THEN it is the writers own retinue

### LlmParameterPolicyTests.cs

_Documents which target models switch the chat-completion request onto the OpenAI reasoning contract (max_completion_tokens, no temperature) and which keep the classic one (max_tokens + temperature)._

- GIVEN the reported model in auto, WHEN resolving, THEN it uses the reasoning contract
- GIVEN various reasoning ids in auto, WHEN detecting, THEN each is recognized
- GIVEN a non reasoning id in auto, WHEN resolving, THEN it keeps the classic contract
- GIVEN ids that merely look similar, WHEN detecting, THEN they are not reasoning models
- GIVEN an empty or null id, WHEN detecting, THEN it is never a reasoning model
- GIVEN standard mode, WHEN resolving a reasoning id, THEN the override forces the classic contract
- GIVEN reasoning mode, WHEN resolving a classic id, THEN the override forces the reasoning contract

### LongTermHistoryPolicyTests.cs

_The world's long-term memory (Gabriel's ruling, 17/10/2026): "Pour le World Events, l'expiration de 42 jours devrait etre pour les evenements recents. Mais apres 42 jours, ca devrait basculer vers une memoire long terme. La memoire long terme servirait entre autre a definir les relations entre NPC.. et pourrait parfois etre utilise pour des conversations entre joueur et NPC sur le passe."_

- GIVEN a skirmish of a few dozen men, WHEN it leaves the news, THEN the world forgets it
- GIVEN a battle of several hundred men, WHEN it leaves the news, THEN it becomes history
- GIVEN a clash of a hundred or two, WHEN it leaves the news, THEN it fades
- GIVEN a siege of a small garrison, WHEN it leaves the news, THEN it becomes history
- GIVEN a battle of unknown size over a settlement, WHEN it leaves the news, THEN it becomes history
- GIVEN a field battle of unknown size, WHEN it leaves the news, THEN it fades
- GIVEN an event the bar names, WHEN it leaves the news, THEN it becomes history
- GIVEN a rising a divorce or a lord fallen in battle, WHEN it leaves the news, THEN it becomes history
- GIVEN a lord fallen in battle, WHEN a lord of his realm with no other tie asks, THEN he knows it
- GIVEN mere gossip, WHEN it leaves the news, THEN it fades
- GIVEN a lord who was in the event, WHEN asked, THEN he knows it
- GIVEN the son of a lord who lost a battle, WHEN asked, THEN he knows it
- GIVEN a lord of the place, WHEN asked, THEN he knows what happened there
- GIVEN one of the greatest events, WHEN a lord of a realm involved is asked, THEN he knows it
- GIVEN a lesser event, WHEN a lord of the realm with no tie to it is asked, THEN he does not know it
- GIVEN a stranger with no tie to the event, WHEN asked, THEN he does not know it
- GIVEN an event the player kept from a lord, WHEN it has become history, THEN he still does not know it
- GIVEN a full history, WHEN one more event arrives, THEN the least of them is forgotten not the greatest
- GIVEN a full history and authored events, WHEN the cap is applied, THEN every authored event stays
- GIVEN a full history of equal events, WHEN one more arrives, THEN the oldest is forgotten
- GIVEN a significant event past the window, WHEN pruned, THEN it is handed to history
- GIVEN a significant event at the window edge, WHEN pruned, THEN it is still news
- GIVEN a skirmish past the window, WHEN pruned, THEN it is simply forgotten
- GIVEN a crowded season, WHEN the news cap pushes out a significant event, THEN it is handed to history
- GIVEN a deed of the player far past the window, WHEN pruned, THEN it stays in the chronicle and nothing is handed off

### LordRecruitmentTests.cs

_The three gates behind join_clan, the most powerful (and most AI-Influence-requested) recruitment: talking a LORD into leaving their own house and clan for the player's, no menu, just conversation. This policy is consulted at BOTH surfaces around the LLM ("the prompt is advice, the bridge is law"): first whether join_clan is even taught to the model, then again whether the action actually executes. Get a gate wrong and either a lord with every reason to stay quietly gets talked into abandoning house and liege, or a genuinely desperate, deeply-trusting, outranked, estranged lord is refused with no visible reason. These tests pin exactly where each of the three gates (Trust, Rank, Vulnerability) sits, and that Vulnerability is an OR of three independent levers, not an AND of all three._

- GIVEN a bond exactly at the trust floor, WHEN evaluating, THEN it passes
- GIVEN a comfortable landed lord in a strong house content with their liege, WHEN evaluating, THEN they will not leave
- GIVEN a lord estranged from their liege, WHEN evaluating, THEN that estrangement makes them recruitable
- GIVEN a lord whose clan is broken, WHEN evaluating, THEN that alone makes them recruitable
- GIVEN a lord with no liege but a low stored liege relation, WHEN evaluating, THEN it does not count as vulnerability
- GIVEN both trust and rank fail, WHEN evaluating, THEN trust is reported first
- GIVEN deep trust a higher rank and a landless lord, WHEN evaluating, THEN they can be recruited
- GIVEN equal clan tiers, WHEN evaluating, THEN rank is not a barrier
- GIVEN estrangement exactly at the threshold, WHEN evaluating, THEN it counts
- GIVEN null facts, WHEN evaluating, THEN it fails closed
- GIVEN only a friendly bond, WHEN evaluating, THEN trust is too low
- GIVEN the player outranked by the lords house, WHEN evaluating, THEN their rank is too low
- GIVEN a lord who leads their own clan, WHEN evaluating, THEN they cannot be recruited even if otherwise eligible
- GIVEN the deep bond mark, WHEN compared with the other two recruitment gates, THEN all three agree

### LordRescuePolicyTests.cs

_Rescue pillar, increment 0 (passive). Decides whether a lord rides to break the captivity the player is sitting in, and on what grounds._

- GIVEN the captivity has not been lived yet, WHEN evaluating, THEN nobody rides
- GIVEN a rescue was already attempted, WHEN evaluating, THEN nobody rides again
- GIVEN the captor is a lord rather than a bandit, WHEN evaluating, THEN nobody rides
- GIVEN the lord cannot ride, WHEN evaluating, THEN nobody rides
- GIVEN the lord is far away, WHEN evaluating, THEN nobody rides
- GIVEN the lord is of the players own clan, WHEN evaluating, THEN he rides as kin
- GIVEN a romantic bond, WHEN evaluating, THEN he rides out of devotion
- GIVEN he holds a grudge against the captors, WHEN evaluating, THEN he rides for his own reasons
- GIVEN an oath binds them, WHEN evaluating, THEN he rides on fealty
- GIVEN an honourable stranger, WHEN evaluating, THEN he rides out of duty
- GIVEN a merciful lord with no honour, WHEN evaluating, THEN he still rides
- GIVEN high personal regard, WHEN evaluating, THEN he rides for the player
- GIVEN a dishonourable stranger who owes the player nothing, WHEN evaluating, THEN he does not come
- GIVEN a lord of no virtue and no warmth, WHEN evaluating, THEN he does not come
- GIVEN several motives at once, WHEN evaluating, THEN the closest bond is the one reported
- GIVEN regard just below the bar, WHEN evaluating, THEN regard is not the reason
- GIVEN the player freed themself first, WHEN the rescue is under way, THEN it is called off
- GIVEN the captors are already gone, WHEN the rescue is under way, THEN it is called off
- GIVEN the rescuer can no longer ride, WHEN the rescue is under way, THEN it is called off
- GIVEN nothing has changed, WHEN the rescue is under way, THEN he keeps coming
- GIVEN no facts at all, WHEN evaluating, THEN nobody rides

### LoverChildClanPolicyTests.cs

_Guards LoverChildClanPolicy (Inc 3c): which line a child conceived by a partner's LOVER joins at birth. The author's hard requirement is that such a child belongs to its genetic father (the lover) and its mother, and NEVER the player-husband. The trap is a wife married INTO the player's clan: the ordinary CR rule places a CR-conceived child in the MOTHER's clan, which for that wife is the player's own house, wrongly adopting her lover's child. These tests pin the "never the player's clan" invariant at every branch, so a future edit that re-lets a lover-child into the player's line fails here instead of shipping. Get it wrong and the player's dynasty silently inherits another man's child._

- GIVEN the lover has his own clan, WHEN resolved, THEN the child joins the lovers line
- GIVEN a clanless lover and a mother in the player clan, WHEN resolved, THEN neither line is used
- GIVEN a clanless lover and a mother in her own non player clan, WHEN resolved, THEN the child joins the mothers line
- GIVEN the lovers clan reads as the player clan, WHEN resolved, THEN it is not used
- GIVEN neither parent has a clan, WHEN resolved, THEN the child is left as is

### LoverConceptionPolicyTests.cs

_Guards LoverConceptionPolicy (Inc 3c): whether a fertile partner conceives by her lover on a tryst. A conception may happen only when the affair is active (a lover is taken), the mother is fertile (the host's PregnancyService.IsEligible read), and a modest per-tryst roll lands. This is the trigger for the lover-child attribution pipeline (the child belongs to the lover, never the player). Get it wrong and either no lover-child is ever conceived (the whole Inc 3c line is dead), or a conception is attempted for a partner with no lover or an infertile mother (an engine conception with no genetic father, or on a woman the engine will never deliver)._

- GIVEN no active affair, WHEN asked, THEN no conception
- GIVEN an infertile mother, WHEN asked, THEN no conception
- GIVEN an active affair and a fertile mother, WHEN the roll lands under the chance, THEN she conceives

### MapEncounterClosePolicyTests.cs

_Whether closing a conversation on the map also finishes the player's encounter. Maifailun (Nexus bug report, 08/10/2026, 2.7.4): "an exception when trying to close the conversation ... i never had this before". His log: he spoke with Dhamshut, a lord whose party was in a fight on the map; on close CR finished the encounter, which settled that fight in the same instant ("[SELF-ESTEEM] Dhamshut: SkirmishWon" written during the close), and the game then drew its map conversation with a leader no longer there (NullReferenceException in MapConversationTableau.SpawnOpponentLeader)._

- GIVEN a plain talk on the open map, WHEN the chat closes, THEN the encounter is finished
- GIVEN a battle under way in the encounter, WHEN the chat closes, THEN the encounter is left to the game
- GIVEN the player raiding a village, WHEN the chat closes, THEN the encounter is finished as before
- GIVEN no map encounter of its own, WHEN the chat closes, THEN nothing is finished
- GIVEN a fight still going, WHEN sighted, THEN the event is live
- GIVEN a finalized event, WHEN sighted, THEN it is not live
- GIVEN an event with a winner, WHEN sighted, THEN it is not live
- GIVEN no event at all, WHEN sighted, THEN it is not live
- GIVEN a talk with a friendly party met on the road, WHEN the chat closes, THEN the meeting is over
- GIVEN the party met is gone, WHEN the chat closes, THEN the meeting is over
- GIVEN a talk with an enemy party, WHEN the chat closes, THEN the encounter menu stays for the player to choose
- GIVEN a talk inside a settlement, WHEN the chat closes, THEN the encounter is not ended
- GIVEN a briefing, WHEN it closes, THEN the meeting goes on
- GIVEN a talk with someone other than the party met, WHEN the chat closes, THEN the meeting is not ended

### MarriageBlockOverridePolicyTests.cs

_EncounterContextBuilder.ResolveMarriageBlockReason consults MarriageBlockOverridePolicy to decide whether a SERVED courtship trial may open a match, and CourtshipTrialBehavior consults IsHardBlock to decide whether a pairing is even eligible to start a trial. These tests pin the ONE line that must never move: a passed trial clears ONLY the soft RegardTooLow gate (the "MARRIAGE NOT YET AN OFFER" the player hit), and can NEVER wave away a HARD blocker the game itself forbids marrying across (an active war, a captive on either side, the player already wed, the same clan, or an unlawful pairing). If this table drifts, a courtship trial could seal a marriage across a war or onto an already-married player, which the whole feature was written to make impossible. Every reason is enumerated explicitly so an added or reordered enum value cannot slip through unclassified._

- GIVEN none, WHEN asked if hard, THEN it is not hard
- GIVEN regard too low, WHEN asked if hard, THEN it is the soft gate and not hard
- GIVEN a hard blocker, WHEN asked if hard, THEN it is hard
- GIVEN the trial is not passed, WHEN asked to override regard, THEN it may not
- GIVEN the trial is passed, WHEN asked to override regard, THEN it may
- GIVEN the trial is passed, WHEN asked to override a hard blocker, THEN it may not

### MarriageClaimPolicyTests.cs

_Marriage-memory audit (player report, Nexus, v1.31.4): a player watched a full wedding narrated in chat and letters, and two [EVENT] entries recorded it ("Mesui and Huan Yi married under Khuzait law...", "...were married before the Khan's envoy..."), yet no marriage ever happened in the engine (cr.marriage_status reported the NPC still free). ProfileMutator.Apply stored the [EVENT] unconditionally, with no check that a matching [ACTION] type: marry had even been emitted, let alone accepted. This policy is the gate: it decides whether a summary's TEXT claims the NPC and the player are wed, so the mod can withhold the event (ChatViewModel.Exchanges.cs) until Hero.Spouse confirms it against real engine state._

- GIVEN the first bug report summary, WHEN checking, THEN it is detected
- GIVEN the second bug report summary, WHEN checking, THEN it is detected
- GIVEN a third partys wedding, WHEN checking, THEN it is not detected
- GIVEN a mere agreement to marry later, WHEN checking, THEN it is not detected
- GIVEN a set wedding date without a sealed vow, WHEN checking, THEN it is not detected
- GIVEN first person plural wedding phrasing, WHEN checking, THEN it is detected
- GIVEN both names present but no marriage word, WHEN checking, THEN it is not detected
- GIVEN a word that merely contains wed as a substring, WHEN checking, THEN it is not detected
- GIVEN null or blank summary, WHEN checking, THEN it is not detected and does not throw
- GIVEN null or blank names, WHEN checking, THEN it is not detected and does not throw

### MarriageEligibilityTests.cs

_Protects the marriage eligibility rule shared by the prompt listing, the bridge's marry re-validation, and love-match eligibility. The adult-age line and the opposite-sex requirement break in silence: a drift either offers impossible matches or refuses every valid one._

- GIVEN an unwed adult of the opposite sex, WHEN checking, THEN they may wed
- GIVEN a deceased candidate, WHEN checking, THEN they cannot wed
- GIVEN a candidate already wed, WHEN checking, THEN they cannot wed
- GIVEN a candidate of the same sex, WHEN checking, THEN they cannot wed
- GIVEN a candidate below marriage age, WHEN checking, THEN they cannot wed
- GIVEN a candidate exactly at marriage age, WHEN checking, THEN they may wed
- GIVEN two adults decades apart, WHEN checking, THEN the age gap is no bar
- GIVEN the player is the far older party, WHEN checking, THEN they may still wed

### MarriageGuardPolicyTests.cs

_Marriage & blessing guards (romance audit M-B3/M-B4/M-B5). These are the "bridge is law" re-validations for the chat marry and blessing actions: without them a hallucinated (or stale-quest) emission could wed a captive or an enemy across an active war, or record a blessing from a dead, imprisoned, at-war, or openly hostile lord, none of which vanilla would ever permit. Each test pins one forbidden state so a future loosening cannot silently reopen a marriage or a blessing that should be impossible._

- GIVEN no captivity and no war, WHEN checking a marriage, THEN it is allowed
- GIVEN any of captivity or war, WHEN checking a marriage, THEN it is forbidden
- GIVEN an arranged match with a captive kin or warring houses, WHEN checking, THEN it is forbidden
- GIVEN a living free peaceful regarding lord, WHEN checking a blessing, THEN it is allowed
- GIVEN any disqualifying state, WHEN checking a blessing, THEN it is forbidden
- GIVEN a blessing for the spouses current family, WHEN marrying, THEN it is honoured
- GIVEN a blessing for a clan the spouse has left, WHEN marrying, THEN it is not honoured
- GIVEN no record or a missing clan id, WHEN marrying, THEN it is not honoured
- GIVEN a marriage the base game arranged, WHEN the family reacts, THEN it approves without any blessing record
- GIVEN a marriage made in conversation, WHEN the family never blessed it, THEN it does not approve

### MarriageProspectPolicyTests.cs

_Protects the line between two questions that used to be one rule: whether a marriage is LAWFUL (MarriageEligibility, no age gap, a contract between two adults) and whether a house would OFFER a given kin of its own accord (this policy, which aims young). It feeds MarriageProspectResolver's prompt listing, the block that tells a clan head which of their kin they may put forward to the player._

- GIVEN a kin past the prospect age, WHEN building an offer, THEN the house does not put them forward
- GIVEN a kin exactly at the prospect age, WHEN building an offer, THEN they are still offered
- GIVEN a kin past the prospect age, WHEN the player asks for them, THEN the marriage is still lawful
- GIVEN a kin barred by law, WHEN building an offer, THEN they are not put forward
- GIVEN a kin below marriage age, WHEN building an offer, THEN they are not put forward

### MarryCompanionEligibilityPolicyTests.cs

_MarryCompanionEligibilityPolicy is the mod-side rule a future SameClan carve-out would consult before letting the player wed one of their own wanderer companions. The whole point of the carve-out is that a hired companion is NOT blood kin, so the incest custom the SameClan bar enforces does not reach them. These tests pin that the rule admits exactly the intended hero and NO other: a companion of compatible sex/orientation, adult, unwed, not blood kin, while the player can still take a spouse. If any refusal silently flipped, the carve-out could marry a blood relative, a second spouse onto an already-wed player, a child, or a corpse, which the SameClan bar was there to stop. Every clause is asserted alone so a reordered or added condition cannot slip through unclassified._

- GIVEN a compatible unwed adult companion and a free player, WHEN asked, THEN they may wed
- GIVEN the hero is not a companion, WHEN asked, THEN refused
- GIVEN a dead companion, WHEN asked, THEN refused
- GIVEN an underage companion, WHEN asked, THEN refused
- GIVEN an orientation incompatible companion, WHEN asked, THEN refused
- GIVEN an already wed companion, WHEN asked, THEN refused
- GIVEN a companion who is blood kin, WHEN asked, THEN refused
- GIVEN the player cannot take another spouse, WHEN asked, THEN refused

### MatchmakerPolicyTests.cs

_fkasad (Nexus, 01/10/2026): "Playing wingman for a guy trying hard to get a girl married?" Gabriel: a REAL marriage, earned in two steps, her heart then her family's consent; never a marriage forced on the family's word alone. If these rules are wrong, a lady is married off to a man she does not care for because her father likes the player, a family gives a daughter to a match it has no reason to accept, or two of one house are "matched"._

- GIVEN she holds him a friend and her family trusts the player, WHEN decided, THEN they are wed
- GIVEN she does not hold him a friend, WHEN decided, THEN she must be won over first
- GIVEN a family with no reason to accept, WHEN decided, THEN it refuses
- GIVEN the suitor of a higher house, WHEN decided, THEN the family agrees on standing alone
- GIVEN two of one house, WHEN decided, THEN it is no match

### MeetingGapDescriberTests.cs

_Documents when the prompt tells an NPC how long it has been since they last saw the player, and how that gap is phrased. The flagship case is the reported one: a companion opening with "Four days since we last spoke..." — a short gap that should NOT be surfaced at all, because map travel eats days quickly._

- GIVEN the reported four day gap, WHEN describing, THEN nothing is surfaced
- GIVEN an unknown or same day gap, WHEN describing, THEN nothing is surfaced
- GIVEN a gap at the one week threshold, WHEN describing, THEN it is still not surfaced
- GIVEN a gap just past a week, WHEN describing, THEN it is surfaced in days
- GIVEN a gap of a season, WHEN describing, THEN it is phrased in seasons
- GIVEN a gap of years, WHEN describing, THEN it is phrased in years
- GIVEN a household member, WHEN describing, THEN the gap is reframed as a shared house
- GIVEN a household member with a fresh gap, WHEN describing, THEN the reassurance is still given
- GIVEN a non household member, WHEN describing, THEN it matches the ordinary note

### MemoryAccentRepairTests.cs

_Shinelegacy (Nexus, 03/10/2026): memories typed at the console before the fix were stored as mojibake ("Ã©" for an e-acute). Gabriel: "est-ce que c'est possible de faire une commande console qui corrige toute la memoire?" The command repairs every stored memory in one pass. If this rule is wrong, a mangled memory stays mangled, a memory that was fine is altered, or a repaired memory loses what it carried besides its words (private, its deed tag)._

- GIVEN one mangled memory and one intact, WHEN repaired, THEN only the mangled one changes
- GIVEN a private tagged memory, WHEN repaired, THEN it stays private tagged and dated
- GIVEN no mangled memory, WHEN repaired, THEN nothing is counted

### MemoryCompressionPolicyTests.cs

_Documents when CR pays for the SDK's LLM-driven memory compressor (LlmMemoryCompressor). Compression is worth an extra LLM call only once an NPC's event history has grown past the threshold, and even then, a fruitless pass (the model kept every event) must not immediately re-trigger, or CR would pay for a fresh call after every single new memory. RetryGrowth caps that cost at one call per handful of new events._

- GIVEN event count below threshold, WHEN never attempted, THEN compression does not run
- GIVEN event count at the threshold, WHEN never attempted, THEN compression runs
- GIVEN a fruitless attempt at the same count, WHEN checking again, THEN compression does not re run
- GIVEN retry growth more events since the fruitless attempt, WHEN checking again, THEN compression runs
- GIVEN a zero or negative threshold, WHEN checking, THEN the default threshold is used instead
- GIVEN a dead character, WHEN checking regardless of history, THEN compression never runs
- GIVEN an alive character, WHEN checking at the same counts, THEN compression runs unchanged

### MemoryDayFormatterTests.cs

_Pins how a memory event's absolute game day is rendered for the player._

- GIVEN a memory dated today, WHEN describing, THEN it reads today
- GIVEN a memory dated yesterday, WHEN describing, THEN it reads yesterday
- GIVEN a memory several days old, WHEN describing, THEN it reads the day count
- GIVEN a future day, WHEN describing, THEN only the bare day is shown
- GIVEN a non positive day, WHEN describing, THEN only the bare day is shown

### MercenaryDeparturePolicyTests.cs

_Documents fkasad's grid for a mercenary's departure (Nexus, 25/09/2026; ratified by Gabriel). The stakes: a company that leaves a realm at peace must never be treated as a traitor (the report that started this: Zivkyn was told he broke an oath he never swore), while one that walks out of a losing war, or straight over to the enemy, must be._

- GIVEN a realm at peace, WHEN the contract ends, THEN there is no fault
- GIVEN a realm winning its war, WHEN the contract ends, THEN the company leaves with thanks
- GIVEN a realm losing its war, WHEN the contract ends, THEN the company abandoned it
- GIVEN a near even war, WHEN judged, THEN the realm is not losing
- GIVEN a company that joins the former employers enemy soon after, WHEN judged, THEN it is a betrayal
- GIVEN an abandonment upgraded to betrayal, WHEN charged, THEN only the difference is added
- GIVEN a company of the realms own people, WHEN it abandons or betrays, THEN the penalty doubles
- GIVEN a company joining its own people in a losing war, WHEN judged, THEN it is aid of kin
- GIVEN a peaceful departure, WHEN worded, THEN it is a contract that ended
- GIVEN a company departure, WHEN asked whether it is news, THEN only abandonment and betrayal are
- GIVEN a company that went over to the enemy, WHEN worded, THEN it names the company the realm and the enemy
- GIVEN a company named with its article, WHEN worded, THEN the article is not doubled
- GIVEN a vassal leaving, WHEN asked whether the grid judges it, THEN only one holding no fief is judged
- GIVEN a sworn vassal, WHEN the departure is charged, THEN the oath weighs like kinship
- GIVEN a sworn vassal leaving a winning war, WHEN charged, THEN there is neither fault nor thanks
- GIVEN a sworn vassals abandonment upgraded to betrayal, WHEN charged, THEN only the difference is added
- GIVEN a sworn vassal, WHEN the departure is worded, THEN it speaks of the oath not of a company
- GIVEN a sworn vassals departure, WHEN its reach is asked, THEN it is never less than a plain departure
- GIVEN a company that rejoins the same realm within days, WHEN the return is judged, THEN it is a renewal
- GIVEN a company that joins another realm or returns late, WHEN judged, THEN it is no renewal
- GIVEN a departure resented, WHEN time passes, THEN the resentment fades to nothing
- GIVEN a resentment, WHEN its remainder is asked, THEN it is never warmer than zero nor harsher than the penalty
- GIVEN the fade, WHEN its length is asked, THEN it is one year of the world

### MercenaryEnlistmentPolicyTests.cs

_join_as_mercenary war-check (v1.34.2 governance hardening, action-system audit): the join_as_mercenary executor enrolled the player's clan under a kingdom's banner with no check on whether the player was AT WAR with that very kingdom, so a player actively fighting a realm could sign on as its hired swords - a state the fiction can never justify. This verifies the two facts both the prompt teaching (ResolveMercenaryOfferKingdom) and the bridge's execution guard (ExecuteJoinAsMercenary) consult, so a refused enrolment is surfaced, never a silent no-op._

- GIVEN the player is free and not at war, WHEN resolving join as mercenary, THEN service is allowed
- GIVEN the player is at war with the kingdom, WHEN resolving join as mercenary, THEN service is refused
- GIVEN the player is not free of a kingdom, WHEN resolving join as mercenary, THEN service is refused

### MercenaryReleasePolicyTests.cs

_end_mercenary fix (player report, 2026-08-04): "The King agreed to free me from my mercenary contract._

- GIVEN the player is under mercenary service, WHEN resolving end mercenary, THEN the release is allowed
- GIVEN the player is not under mercenary service, WHEN resolving end mercenary, THEN the release is refused

### ModRecoveryPolicyTests.cs

_ModRecoveryPolicy backs the cr.reset recovery command, offered to a player whose mod stopped working after a council scene soft-locked and they force-quit. The command must stand down exactly the stuck subsystems and report them honestly, and must NEVER over-report (claiming it reset something that was fine) or under-report (leaving the player thinking nothing happened when it did). These tests pin the plan for each flag combination and the wording, including the "nothing was stuck" case which must still reassure and point at the log._

- GIVEN both a chat overlay and an active assembly, WHEN planning, THEN both are stood down overlay first
- GIVEN only a chat overlay, WHEN planning, THEN only the overlay is closed
- GIVEN only an active assembly, WHEN planning, THEN only the assembly is cancelled
- GIVEN nothing stuck, WHEN planning, THEN the plan is empty
- GIVEN an empty plan, WHEN describing, THEN it reassures and points at the log
- GIVEN a full plan, WHEN describing, THEN it names each cleared subsystem
- GIVEN a null plan, WHEN describing, THEN it does not throw and reassures

### ModderReadingsTests.cs

_What tashmetu's Regard and Standing (Nexus, 08/10/2026) needs from CR's public door, ratified by Gabriel: - its memories of nobles' own lives ("I have learned that my wife...") filed as OwnLife, kept apart from the player; - how strictly a culture holds marriage, which it read from CR's internal JealousyCultureLoader: any rename here would have broken it silently. It is now a public reading with the same answer._

- GIVEN a mod filing a nobles own affair, WHEN injected as own life, THEN it is kept as own life
- GIVEN a strictly monogamous culture, WHEN read, THEN it is strict and never shares
- GIVEN a lenient culture, WHEN read at and below its elite tier, THEN sharing follows the tier

### ModelModePolicyTests.cs

_Documents the Phase 2 model routing: three configured models (PROSE / NSFW / INTERPRET) and two composition modes (STANDARD = one integrated call routed by content, ENHANCED = PROSE writes + INTERPRET tags + NSFW hard-failure fallback)._

- GIVEN nsfw state, WHEN resolving the main role, THEN it uses nsfw
- GIVEN roleplay state, WHEN resolving the main role, THEN it uses prose
- GIVEN enhanced state, WHEN resolving the main role, THEN it uses prose
- GIVEN a manual state, WHEN resolving composition, THEN only enhanced yields enhanced composition
- GIVEN each manual state, WHEN asking its display name, THEN it names the model
- GIVEN the manual state, WHEN cycling, THEN it advances enhanced roleplay nsfw and wraps
- GIVEN configured models, WHEN resolving a role, THEN each role yields its own id
- GIVEN a blank explicit or interpret model, WHEN resolving, THEN it falls back to prose
- GIVEN a mode, WHEN asked about interpreter and fallback, THEN only enhanced uses them
- GIVEN reasoning efforts, WHEN resolving, THEN each role uses its own or falls back to prose
- GIVEN provider pins, WHEN resolving, THEN each role uses its own or falls back to prose

### ModuleRootPathTests.cs

_Where the mod reads its own files from. EmperorHaterius (Nexus, 04/10/2026): his CR log said "not found" for every file the mod ships (world.txt, character_overrides.json, cultural_traditions.json, jealousy_cultures.json, the guidelines) and the council button art could not be built, all from "../../Modules\CalradiaRemembers\...", while the chat prefabs, found through the engine's own module path, loaded. The Dark Leather window was see-through, with no way out, because its art lived on the same broken path. The engine knows where the module really is; a path rebuilt by hand from BasePath.Name only holds when the module sits in the game's own Modules folder AND the process started in its bin folder. If this guard fails, a player whose install differs plays with none of the mod's files._

- GIVEN the mod source, WHEN it builds a path to its own files, THEN it asks the engine where the module is

### NameNormalizationTests.cs

_Documents the diacritic-folding fix: a player typing "Heloise" for a hero named "Héloïse the Finger" was a silent name-match failure. Fold() is the single pure seam both name-matching sites now share._

- GIVEN an accented name, WHEN folding, THEN diacritics are stripped and lowercased
- GIVEN an already plain name, WHEN folding, THEN it is only lowercased
- GIVEN mixed case input, WHEN folding, THEN the result is fully lowercase
- GIVEN null or empty input, WHEN folding, THEN an empty string is returned
- GIVEN diacritics from several languages, WHEN folding, THEN each is stripped
- GIVEN a full epithet name, WHEN folding, THEN spaces and word order are preserved

### NativeQuestMemoryTests.cs

_The game's own quests, remembered by the one who set them. tashmetu and Maifailun (Nexus, 08/10/2026): accepting a native quest closes the conversation in the game's own dialogue, and nothing of it was kept: the lord who asked for it, spoken to later, only knew it while it was still open, and nothing at all once it was done or failed ("Maifailun would love quest memories from it"). Gabriel ratified a memory for the giver when such a quest begins and when it ends, worded by how it ended. If this breaks, a lord forgets the errand he set, or thanks the player for one failed._

- GIVEN a native quest accepted, WHEN remembered, THEN the giver recalls asking for it
- GIVEN a native quest ended, WHEN remembered, THEN the memory follows how it ended
- GIVEN a quest the game voided, WHEN remembered, THEN nothing is written

### NemesisBountyPolicyTests.cs

_Nemesis pillar increment 3 (bounty missions, the world hook): the pure eligibility gate deciding whether a given lord may offer the player a bounty on a given tracked nemesis._

- GIVEN the lords home settlement matches the nemesis region, WHEN checking, THEN it matches
- GIVEN the lord has no home settlement but stands in the nemesis region, WHEN checking, THEN it matches
- GIVEN no settlement keys but the lords culture matches the nemesis region, WHEN checking, THEN it matches
- GIVEN none of the lords keys match, WHEN checking, THEN it does not match
- GIVEN the nemesis has no region id, WHEN checking, THEN it never matches
- GIVEN the lord has no keys at all, WHEN checking, THEN it does not match
- GIVEN a living unturned nemesis this lord has not bountied in a matching region, WHEN evaluating, THEN eligible
- GIVEN the nemesis is already dead, WHEN evaluating, THEN the lord is never eligible
- GIVEN the nemesis has been turned, WHEN evaluating, THEN no lord may bounty him
- GIVEN this lord already bountied him, WHEN evaluating, THEN the same lord may not offer a second
- GIVEN the lords region does not match the nemesis, WHEN evaluating, THEN the lord is not eligible

### NemesisBountyRewardPolicyTests.cs

_Nemesis pillar increment 3 (bounty missions): the pure gold/relation math for a lord's bounty on a tracked nemesis, scaled by his notoriety (RecaptureCount, TimesEncountered)._

- GIVEN a nemesis never recaptured nor encountered, WHEN computing gold, THEN it is the base amount
- GIVEN prior recaptures, WHEN computing gold, THEN each one adds its own share
- GIVEN prior encounters with no recapture, WHEN computing gold, THEN each one adds its own smaller share
- GIVEN a highly notorious nemesis, WHEN computing gold, THEN it is capped at the ceiling
- GIVEN growing notoriety, WHEN computing gold, THEN it never decreases
- GIVEN negative counters, WHEN computing gold, THEN they are treated as zero not subtracted
- GIVEN a nemesis never recaptured, WHEN computing relation, THEN it is the base amount
