- GIVEN no matching prisoner bargain exists, WHEN building the message, THEN it denies the handoff
- GIVEN give item named no item at all, WHEN building the message, THEN it is a parenthetical in character note
- GIVEN grant blessing named no one, WHEN building the message, THEN it is a parenthetical in character note
- GIVEN arrange marriage is missing a kin name, WHEN building the message, THEN it is a parenthetical in character note
- GIVEN the named hero is not the player spouse, WHEN building the message, THEN it denies a marriage exists
- GIVEN no divorce demand is pending, WHEN building the message, THEN it names the spouse and denies a demand
- GIVEN teach skill named no skill at all, WHEN building the message, THEN it is a parenthetical in character note

### SkirmishMemoryTextTests.cs

_Decides what a companion remembers of the small fights shared with the player: those the chronicle does not record (no siege, no armies, under 150 men, or no lord leading either side). Thoragoros1 (Nexus, 15/10/2026) fought bandits, a patrol and lords' parties with three companions and none of them remembered any of it; Gabriel ruled that a companion remembers every fight shared with the player. If this is wrong, either the companions forget again, or a looter-hunting campaign writes one memory per fight into every companion's profile (the save bloat the folding exists to prevent), or a folded line swallows a real battle memory._

- GIVEN one small fight, WHEN writing the memory, THEN the companion remembers who was fought and how it went
- GIVEN a lost small fight, WHEN writing the memory, THEN it says it was lost
- GIVEN several small fights, WHEN writing the memory, THEN one line counts them and tells the last
- GIVEN a line the fold wrote, WHEN reading its count, THEN it reads back exactly the count written
- GIVEN a notable battle memory, WHEN a small fight ends, THEN it is never folded into
- GIVEN a recent small fight line, WHEN another small fight ends, THEN it folds into that line
- GIVEN an old small fight line, WHEN another small fight ends, THEN a new line starts
- GIVEN a line at the cap, WHEN another small fight ends, THEN a new line starts
- GIVEN each kind of foe, WHEN naming it, THEN a lord bandits and a realm read as themselves

### SpeakerNamesakePolicyTests.cs

_that saying the name of the person in front of you does not summon their namesakes._

- GIVEN a match made only of the speakers own name, WHEN judged, THEN it is a namesake
- GIVEN a match carrying one word the speaker does not have, WHEN judged, THEN it stands
- GIVEN a match sharing nothing with the speaker, WHEN judged, THEN it stands
- GIVEN a two part speaker name, WHEN the match uses only those parts, THEN it is a namesake
- GIVEN a match in another case, WHEN judged, THEN it is still a namesake
- GIVEN nothing to judge on, WHEN judged, THEN nothing is dropped

### SpeakerProfileSelectorTests.cs

_WHY IT MATTERS: this is the exact fix for a reported bug ("my captor thinks he's my companion Fercread" / "Garios thinks he's Fercread", CHANGELOG + ChatViewModel.MainSpeaker) where a captor's dialogue and memories bled from the LAST companion the player had chatted with, because the old code consulted the session-lifetime DialogFlow.Profile FIRST. Select() feeds ChatViewModel.MainSpeaker() every turn, so whichever profile it returns is the one the LLM prompt is built from AND the one any [EVENT]/relation change gets written back onto. Get the precedence wrong and a scene's events land on the wrong person's memory, permanently._

- (not stated as a rule) Given the speaker hero has their own profile When selecting Then it always wins over every cache
- (not stated as a rule) Given a heroless captive encounter When selecting Then the captive profile wins not the last conversation
- (not stated as a rule) Given a heroless captor scene When selecting Then the captor scene profile is used
- (not stated as a rule) Given an audience When selecting Then the audience profile outranks the other modes
- (not stated as a rule) Given an ordinary conversation with no hero profile yet When selecting Then the last conversation profile is the final fallback

### SpeakerProfileSourceTests.cs

_The chat's partner's profile must be the partner's own. NpcMemoryCampaignBehavior.DialogFlow.Profile is a session-lifetime cache: the LAST person reached through the game's dialogue menu. Read as "the current partner" it sent work to the wrong ledger three times: a captor scene wore another hero's memories ("Garios thinks he's Fercread"), a captor scene's summary landed on the last person chatted with (Nemesis audit RC2), and on 02/10/2026 the offer bag looked for a companion's supply task in someone else's profile (Lordfadooboo: "a companion asked me to deliver 20 sacks of grain... the quest wouldnt complete"). The one legitimate read is MainSpeaker's last-resort fallback, behind every live resolution. If this guard is wrong, a fourth path quietly reads the cache and some player's deed is credited to, or refused by, the wrong person._

- GIVEN the mod source, WHEN a conversation partners profile is read, THEN only the speaker selectors fallback reads the dialog cache

### SpokenFigureTests.cs

_The last sum a speaker named, read from what they said, in digits or in words._

- GIVEN a sum written in words, WHEN read, THEN it is the figure
- GIVEN a sum written in digits, WHEN read, THEN it is the figure
- GIVEN several sums, WHEN read, THEN the last one is the offer
- GIVEN common spoken sums, WHEN read, THEN each is the figure
- GIVEN only small numbers, WHEN read with a minimum, THEN there is no sum

### SpouseDivorceDemandPolicyTests.cs

_Divorce, Phase 2b: the player's OWN spouse, unprompted, decides whether to raise a divorce demand, entirely through conversation (no UI button). ShouldDemand decides WHEN that first demand fires; ShouldEscalate decides whether a refused-enough spouse stops asking and BEGINS an estrangement on a deadline despite the player; IsReconciled decides whether that estrangement gets called off. Get ShouldDemand wrong and either a happy marriage starts pestering the player with unbidden divorce letters, or a miserable one sits mute forever. Get the happy-marriage veto wrong (it reads the mod's OWN personal regard, NpcProfile.ReputationWithPlayer, not vanilla relation) and a spouse the player has genuinely won over still gets hounded by a stale grudge or a lagging vanilla number. Get the pregnant/prisoner gates or the reconciliation hysteresis wrong and a marriage ends, or an estrangement is called off, at a moment the story explicitly forbids._

- GIVEN a happy marriage, WHEN evaluating, THEN no demand is raised
- GIVEN a miserable relation, WHEN evaluating, THEN a demand is raised
- GIVEN a fresh jealousy wound, WHEN the relation is only moderate, THEN a demand is still raised
- GIVEN a freshly discovered bastard scandal, WHEN the relation is only moderate, THEN a demand is still raised
- GIVEN a live grudge above the bar, WHEN the relation is only moderate, THEN a demand is raised
- GIVEN a live grudge below the bar, WHEN nothing else tips it, THEN no demand is raised
- GIVEN a pregnancy in play, WHEN the marriage is miserable, THEN no demand is raised
- GIVEN either party held prisoner, WHEN the marriage is miserable, THEN no demand is raised
- GIVEN a demand already pending, WHEN the marriage is miserable, THEN it is never re raised
- GIVEN null facts, WHEN evaluating, THEN no demand is raised
- GIVEN a miserable relation, WHEN personal regard is high, THEN the demand is vetoed
- GIVEN a fresh jealousy wound, WHEN personal regard is high, THEN the demand is vetoed
- GIVEN a live grudge above the bar, WHEN personal regard is high, THEN the demand is vetoed
- GIVEN personal regard one below the veto threshold, WHEN the marriage is miserable, THEN the demand still fires
- GIVEN personal regard at the veto threshold, WHEN the marriage is miserable, THEN the demand is vetoed
- GIVEN enough refusals, WHEN the marriage is still unhappy, THEN the spouse escalates
- GIVEN one fewer refusal than the bar, WHEN the marriage is still unhappy, THEN no escalation
- GIVEN enough refusals, WHEN the relation has since recovered above the misery bar, THEN no escalation
- GIVEN more than enough refusals, WHEN the marriage is still unhappy, THEN the spouse escalates
- GIVEN an estrangement already under way, WHEN evaluating, THEN it is never begun twice
- GIVEN a pregnancy in play, WHEN the refusal bar is cleared, THEN no escalation
- GIVEN either party held prisoner, WHEN the refusal bar is cleared, THEN no escalation
- GIVEN null facts, WHEN evaluating escalation, THEN no escalation is raised
- GIVEN enough refusals, WHEN personal regard is high, THEN the escalation is vetoed
- GIVEN personal regard one below the veto threshold, WHEN the refusal bar is cleared, THEN the escalation still fires
- GIVEN personal regard at the veto threshold, WHEN the refusal bar is cleared, THEN the escalation is vetoed
- GIVEN relation at the reconciliation threshold, WHEN checking reconciliation, THEN it is reconciled
- GIVEN relation one below the reconciliation threshold, WHEN checking reconciliation, THEN it is not reconciled
- GIVEN relation merely clearing the misery bar, WHEN checking reconciliation, THEN it is not reconciled

### SpouseTenurePolicyTests.cs

_raphareish (Nexus, 01/10/2026): a character just married (the notification was in the chat log) spoke of her spouse as if they had been married for years. The family line now says how recent the wedding is. If this rule is wrong, a bride of three days reminisces about years of marriage, or a couple wed long ago is called newlyweds (or, fkasad 10/10/2026, a mother of three thinks she wed last week)._

- GIVEN a wedding days ago, WHEN the spouse is named, THEN they are newlyweds
- GIVEN a wedding this year, WHEN the spouse is named, THEN it is still young
- GIVEN an old or unknown wedding, WHEN the spouse is named, THEN nothing is said
- GIVEN an unseen wedding and a child of some years, WHEN the spouse is named, THEN the marriage reads as years old
- GIVEN no child or a very young one, WHEN the spouse is named, THEN no length is claimed

### StagedConversationStartTests.cs

_When a staged scene starts the game's conversation itself. Gabriel's play test (08/10/2026): the post-duel scene opened in the town square with Tharos brought beside the player, but the game's conversation with him never began within the second the stager waits, so the chat opened over the scene with no conversation: Tharos, held by nothing, walked off, and the game's own greeting came ten seconds later instead of the post-duel talk. When the scene is up and the person stands in it but no conversation has begun, the stager begins it itself, once, as the game does when one clicks on someone; only if that fails does the bare fallback open the chat._

- GIVEN the person in the scene and no conversation, WHEN the grace runs out, THEN the stager begins it
- GIVEN a conversation live a try made no one there or a map conversation, WHEN asked, THEN it does not

### StagingGracePolicyTests.cs

_that a meeting staged inside a settlement is not judged by the clock of a meeting staged on the map._

- GIVEN a staging waiting on an interior scene, WHEN asked, THEN it is given far longer than a map one
- GIVEN a staging waiting on an interior scene, WHEN asked, THEN it outlasts a slow load
- GIVEN a staging waiting on a map conversation, WHEN asked, THEN it gives up quickly
- GIVEN either kind of staging, WHEN asked, THEN the wait is finite and positive

### StagingHostPolicyTests.cs

_Where a CR conversation opens. Gabriel's rule: never a screen over the LIVE map, which keeps ticking underneath (the siege-bombardment native crash, the stolen hotkeys, the VRAM leak). A conversation opens over a live scene, or a scene is staged for it. At sea no scene can load (the endless loading screen of 1.30.16), and until 2.6.7 the chat then opened straight over the naval map. Windmolen (Nexus, 28/09/2026): "when on the move, if I accept the envoy's invitation, the chat window opens but flickers a few seconds and then my game crashes. The last time it was a naval move." At sea, a conversation now waits for landfall; and an envoy, who rides, does not arrive until then._

- GIVEN the player at sea with no scene live, WHEN a conversation is asked for, THEN it waits for landfall
- GIVEN the player on land with no scene live, WHEN a conversation is asked for, THEN a scene is staged
- GIVEN a scene already live, WHEN a conversation is asked for, THEN it opens over it even at sea
- GIVEN a conversation waiting for landfall, WHEN asked whether it may be received, THEN only ashore and with the screen free
- GIVEN the ship just docked, WHEN a waiting conversation would be received, THEN it waits for the landing to settle
- GIVEN the player at sea, WHEN an envoy would arrive, THEN he waits for landfall

### StanceBearingTextTests.cs

_The "Bearing" encyclopedia line: numberless Trust/Respect/Fear phrasing (NOT Affection — see StanceBearingText's own header for why)._

- GIVEN strong positive trust, WHEN phrasing, THEN it reads trusts your word
- GIVEN strong negative trust, WHEN phrasing, THEN it reads does not trust your word
- GIVEN neutral trust, WHEN phrasing, THEN it is null
- GIVEN strong positive respect, WHEN phrasing, THEN it reads respects your strength
- GIVEN strong negative respect, WHEN phrasing, THEN it reads holds you in contempt
- GIVEN neutral respect, WHEN phrasing, THEN it is null
- GIVEN high fear, WHEN phrasing, THEN it reads fears you
- GIVEN moderate fear, WHEN phrasing, THEN it reads is wary of you
- GIVEN mild fear, WHEN phrasing, THEN it reads is uneasy around you
- GIVEN no fear, WHEN phrasing, THEN it is null
- GIVEN every axis wholly neutral, WHEN composing, THEN it is an empty string not null
- GIVEN only fear stands out, WHEN composing, THEN only the fear phrase appears
- GIVEN every axis stands out, WHEN composing, THEN all three join in trust respect fear order
- GIVEN a believable wary but unafraid combination, WHEN composing, THEN it reads naturally

### StanceConsequencePolicyTests.cs

_Documents who dares to do what as a posture hardens: mild dislike is a cold shoulder, real hostility a threat, and deep hatred turns to a plot / an open challenge / an assassination ONLY for the character that fits — honour stays a lord's hand from murder and scheming, and terror of the player rules out striking in person._

- GIVEN a neutral stance, WHEN resolving, THEN nothing is warranted
- GIVEN mild dislike, WHEN resolving, THEN a cold shoulder
- GIVEN real hostility, WHEN resolving, THEN a threat
- GIVEN deep hatred in a cold cruel schemer, WHEN resolving, THEN an assassination
- GIVEN deep hatred in a cold schemer with a scruple, WHEN resolving, THEN a plot not murder
- GIVEN deep hatred in a bold warrior, WHEN resolving, THEN an open challenge
- GIVEN deep hatred but terror of the player, WHEN resolving, THEN no strike in person
- GIVEN deep hatred in an honourable lord, WHEN resolving, THEN he will not scheme or murder
- GIVEN mild warmth, WHEN resolving, THEN a warm welcome
- GIVEN strong warmth and respect, WHEN resolving, THEN a favor
- GIVEN strong warmth and deep trust, WHEN resolving, THEN a warning of a plot
- GIVEN warm affection but collapsed trust, WHEN resolving, THEN no warm welcome
- GIVEN warm affection with trust just above the floor, WHEN resolving, THEN still a warm welcome

### StanceDescriptorTests.cs

_Protects how a stance reads in the prompt — per-axis tiers, and that believable COMBINATIONS compose correctly (the whole point of the multi-axis system). A drift would have NPCs misread their own posture, or voice a neutral feeling as a strong one._

- GIVEN warmth across the range, WHEN phrasing affection, THEN each tier reads right
- GIVEN trust across the range, WHEN phrasing, THEN each tier reads right
- GIVEN respect across the range, WHEN phrasing, THEN each tier reads right
- GIVEN fear across the range, WHEN phrasing, THEN it is unipolar
- GIVEN a wholly neutral npc, WHEN summarizing, THEN the block is skipped
- GIVEN a mixed stance, WHEN summarizing, THEN only the axes that matter are voiced
- GIVEN a beaten rival, WHEN summarizing, THEN respect and fear coexist without restating affection
- GIVEN a charming rogue the lord likes but cannot trust, WHEN summarizing, THEN distrust and respect show without restating warmth
- GIVEN a feared but unloved overlord, WHEN summarizing, THEN fear and respect carry without warmth

### StanceGatePolicyTests.cs

_Stance mechanical behaviour-gating, increment 1: protects the Fear-coercion thresholds ratified 2026-07-04 — a terrified captive yields softer terms but plants a HIDDEN grudge instead of open regard, Honor/Valor stiffen resolve, Calculating breaks sooner and resents it more, and an unafraid, contemptuous captive refuses outright. Gabriel reads these as the tuning's documentation._

- GIVEN a terrified captive with neutral traits, WHEN resolving coercion, THEN they yield softer terms with a hidden grudge
- GIVEN exactly the yield fear floor, WHEN resolving coercion, THEN it still counts as afraid enough
- GIVEN one fear point below the floor, WHEN resolving coercion, THEN they do not yield
- GIVEN an honourable and valorous captive, WHEN the same fear no longer crosses the raised floor, THEN they stay neutral not yielding
- GIVEN an honourable and valorous captive, WHEN fear climbs past their raised floor, THEN they still eventually yield
- GIVEN a calculating captive, WHEN resolving coercion, THEN they yield at a lower fear than a neutral captive would
- GIVEN a calculating captive, WHEN they yield, THEN the hidden grudge carries a bigger magnitude
- GIVEN a negative calculating trait, WHEN resolving coercion, THEN it does not raise the floor the wrong way
- GIVEN an unafraid and contemptuous captive, WHEN resolving coercion, THEN they refuse flat
- GIVEN fear exactly at the refuse ceiling, WHEN resolving coercion, THEN it no longer qualifies as unafraid enough to refuse
- GIVEN respect exactly at the refuse ceiling, WHEN resolving coercion, THEN it no longer qualifies as contemptuous enough to refuse
- GIVEN low fear but meaningful respect, WHEN resolving coercion, THEN they do not refuse outright
- GIVEN a mid range captive, WHEN resolving coercion, THEN the outcome stays neutral
- GIVEN extreme honor and valor, WHEN resolving coercion, THEN the effective floor never exceeds full fear
- GIVEN trust above the delegated risk floor, WHEN checking delegated risk, THEN the npc will delegate risk
- GIVEN trust exactly at the delegated risk floor, WHEN checking delegated risk, THEN it still counts as trusted enough
- GIVEN trust one point below the floor and no relation nor loyalty, WHEN checking delegated risk, THEN the npc will not delegate risk
- GIVEN neutral trust and no relation nor loyalty, WHEN checking delegated risk, THEN the npc will not delegate risk
- GIVEN deeply distrustful trust and no relation nor loyalty, WHEN checking delegated risk, THEN the npc will not delegate risk
- GIVEN zero trust but an established high relation, WHEN checking delegated risk, THEN the established ally still lends
- GIVEN zero trust and a relation one point below the bypass and no loyalty, WHEN checking delegated risk, THEN the npc will not delegate risk
- GIVEN no trust and a cold clan standing but high personal affection, WHEN checking delegated risk, THEN personal loyalty carries the favour
- GIVEN affection one point below the loyalty floor and no other basis, WHEN checking delegated risk, THEN the npc will not delegate risk
- GIVEN respect above the floor, WHEN checking commands respect, THEN the player s weight carries
- GIVEN respect exactly at the floor, WHEN checking commands respect, THEN it still counts as esteemed enough
- GIVEN respect one point below the floor and no established relation, WHEN checking commands respect, THEN the player s weight does not carry
- GIVEN neutral respect and no established relation, WHEN checking commands respect, THEN the player s weight does not carry
- GIVEN deeply contemptuous respect and no established relation, WHEN checking commands respect, THEN the player s weight does not carry
- GIVEN zero respect but an established high relation, WHEN checking commands respect, THEN the established ally still carries the player s weight
- GIVEN zero respect and a relation one point below the bypass, WHEN checking commands respect, THEN the player s weight does not carry
- GIVEN a merely eligible lord with respect above the floor, WHEN checking join clan, THEN the lord will join
- GIVEN a merely eligible lord who does not respect the player, WHEN checking join clan, THEN the lord refuses despite the recruitment relation
- GIVEN a lord just below the recruit bypass with no respect, WHEN checking join clan, THEN the lord still refuses
- GIVEN an adored lord at the recruit bypass with no respect, WHEN checking join clan, THEN the lord joins on relation alone

### StanceGateTests.cs

_Moves the Trust/Respect/Fear axes that StanceGatePolicy later gates real actions on: prisoner-release coercion (Fear), lending troops/influence (Trust), joining the player's clan (Respect). The caps here are the WHOLE anti-exploit guarantee for that machinery: WordCap keeps a single chat exchange from ever reaching a gate threshold on its own (per the ROADMAP's own math, walking Fear to 40 by words alone would take ~20 accepted rounds over ~160 in-game hours), while DeedCap lets one verified deed move a stance decisively. Get a cap, the cooldown, or a clamp bound wrong here, and the LLM can talk its way past a gate meant to require proof._

- GIVEN a bipolar axis, WHEN clamping, THEN it is held within minus hundred and hundred
- GIVEN a deed pushing past the axis limit, WHEN applied, THEN it clamps
- GIVEN a deed, WHEN applied, THEN it ignores the word cooldown
- GIVEN a persuasive speech, WHEN a word round is applied, THEN each axis moves only within the word cap
- GIVEN a proven deed, WHEN applied, THEN it moves the axis strongly
- GIVEN a recent word round, WHEN speaking again within the cooldown, THEN it is refused
- GIVEN a word round that says nothing, WHEN applied, THEN it is a no op and does not start the cooldown
- GIVEN an outsized deed delta, WHEN applied, THEN it is bounded by the deed cap
- GIVEN long calm, WHEN fear decays, THEN it never falls below zero
- GIVEN no fear or no days, WHEN decaying, THEN nothing happens
- GIVEN standing on both sides of zero, WHEN decaying, THEN each steps one toward zero
- GIVEN trust and respect already zero, WHEN decaying, THEN nothing moves
- GIVEN the cooldown has passed, WHEN speaking again, THEN a new round lands
- GIVEN the fear axis, WHEN clamping, THEN it is unipolar zero to hundred
- GIVEN time passing, WHEN fear decays, THEN it fades toward calm
- GIVEN words against a settled judgement, WHEN compared to a deed, THEN the deed dwarfs them

### StanceParsingTests.cs

_Protects parsing of the LLM's [STANCE] block into a StanceShiftData — the spoken nudges to trust/respect/fear. A parsing drift silently drops the player's spoken influence, or leaks the block into the displayed line._

- GIVEN a stance block, WHEN parsing, THEN the signed deltas are read
- GIVEN no stance block, WHEN parsing, THEN there is no shift
- GIVEN an all zero stance block, WHEN parsing, THEN it is treated as nothing
- GIVEN a stance block, WHEN parsing, THEN it does not leak into the dialogue
- GIVEN only one axis moved, WHEN parsing, THEN the others default to zero

### StationGuidelinesPolicyTests.cs

_Station-matched behavior guidelines: occupation -> register mapping + the text fallback chain._

- GIVEN a gang leader, WHEN resolving the station, THEN the street register applies
- GIVEN a bandit hero, WHEN resolving the station, THEN the street register applies too
- GIVEN town and village notables, WHEN resolving the station, THEN they share the notable register
- GIVEN a wanderer, WHEN resolving the station, THEN the free blade register applies
- GIVEN a lord or an unknown occupation, WHEN resolving the station, THEN the base register is kept
- GIVEN a wanderer who joined the player, WHEN resolving the station, THEN the companion register applies
- GIVEN a companion granted a fief, WHEN resolving the station, THEN it rises to the lordly base
- GIVEN no promotion signals, WHEN resolving the station, THEN it matches the plain occupation mapping
- GIVEN the station file exists, WHEN selecting the text, THEN it is returned
- GIVEN the gangleader file is missing, WHEN selecting the text, THEN it falls back to the notable register
- GIVEN the companion file is missing, WHEN selecting the text, THEN it falls back to the wanderer register
- GIVEN the companion file exists, WHEN selecting the text, THEN it is used
- GIVEN no station file exists, WHEN selecting the text, THEN null means use the base guidelines
- GIVEN a blank station file, WHEN selecting the text, THEN it falls through instead of blanking the prompt

### StatureTests.cs

_Rank as a type (Gabriel, 28/09/2026). The engine-facts map found clan tier read by 28 rules under five names, each with its own marks; Stature gives the marks one home. It is a MOVE, not a retuning: every rung is a mark a rule already used, and the duel's station comes out of ChatViewModel unchanged. If a rung drifts, the rules that point at it move together, which is the point; if the station drifts, a duel is refused (or fought) across a gap it was not before._

- GIVEN a clan tier and rulership, WHEN the duel station is read, THEN it is as it always was
- GIVEN two statures, WHEN the station gap is read, THEN it counts only how far the lower stands beneath
- GIVEN the named rungs, WHEN read, THEN they are the marks the rules already used
- GIVEN a negative tier, WHEN a stature is made, THEN it reads as no standing

### StolenSpouseGrudgePolicyTests.cs

_Stolen Love pillar, Increment 3: sizes the jilted spouse's grudge when the player's affair ends their marriage. The behavior plants this as a GrudgeSource.StolenSpouse grudge against the player; it feeds nemesis and the vanilla Blood Feuds. If it drifts, being left for the player either barely registers (no consequence to the betrayal, so the pillar has no bite) or plants a life-sentence no amends could outlive (the grudge system is meant to let deeds win someone round in time, which the cap protects)._

- GIVEN a neutral marriage, WHEN sizing the grudge, THEN it is the baseline sting
- GIVEN a dearly loved partner lost, WHEN sizing the grudge, THEN it cuts deeper
- GIVEN a merciful spouse, WHEN sizing the grudge, THEN it is softened
- GIVEN a very merciful spouse, WHEN sizing the grudge, THEN it never falls below the floor
- GIVEN a devoted spouse losing a beloved, WHEN sizing the grudge, THEN it stops at the winnable cap

### StrategicAssessmentTests.cs

_WHY IT MATTERS: the Strategic Assessment is a COMPANION strategist counselling the player from the World Almanac, and its whole value rests on two rules that are invisible once it is LLM prose: the counsel's QUALITY must scale with the advisor's Tactics skill (the player's incentive to keep a good strategist), and the advisor must be grounded in the REAL standings and the player's own position while never being licensed to invent a fact. Both live in the pure prompt this builds; if the persona, the quality dial, the facts, the player's frame, or the no-invention rule ever fell out of the prompt, the feature would silently drift into a generic, ungrounded blurb._

- GIVEN tactics levels, WHEN tiering, THEN each boundary lands in the right tier
- GIVEN the tiers, WHEN reading guidance, THEN each is present and a master differs from a green
- GIVEN an advisor, WHEN building the prompt, THEN it names them and their culture
- GIVEN a master versus a green advisor, WHEN building, THEN the quality guidance matches the skill
- GIVEN the world standings, WHEN building, THEN real kingdoms are laid out and invention is forbidden
- GIVEN a player with a realm at war, WHEN building, THEN their kingdom rank and wars frame the counsel
- GIVEN a landless player, WHEN building, THEN the counsel reframes for a captain with no realm
- GIVEN any inputs, WHEN building, THEN it asks for advice not a recital in a few sentences
- GIVEN any inputs, WHEN building, THEN it pins second person to the lord and self actions to third
- GIVEN active wars, WHEN building, THEN the real belligerents are named
- GIVEN no wars, WHEN building, THEN it states the realms are at peace
- GIVEN a realm at war on several fronts, WHEN building, THEN each war is named once grouped by realm
- GIVEN a realm that has not fought, WHEN building, THEN it reads as unfought never as losing
- GIVEN the players own world files, WHEN building, THEN the counsel is given in that world
- GIVEN no battle ledger, WHEN building, THEN the advisor is forbidden to invent a war history
- GIVEN a companion with a persona, WHEN building, THEN it speaks in their character
- GIVEN a narrative style, WHEN building, THEN it is injected and capped
- GIVEN an empty world, WHEN building, THEN it produces a prompt without throwing
- GIVEN no advisor name, WHEN building, THEN it falls back to the war council
- GIVEN a common soldier advisor, WHEN building, THEN it voices a plain soldier not a strategist
- GIVEN the standings, WHEN summarising offline, THEN it states facts with no advisor or task
- GIVEN an empty world, WHEN summarising offline, THEN it reports no reigning power

### SuccessionRulesTests.cs

_Guards dynastic succession's two pure rules: the predecessor's private history does NOT cross to the heir (but their political/relational history does), and the kinship word is chosen correctly._

- GIVEN a profile with private and public history, WHEN reframed, THEN only the public survives and the inheritance is named
- GIVEN a predecessor who retired, WHEN reframed, THEN the profile says so
- GIVEN a private event, WHEN succeeding, THEN it does not cross to the heir
- GIVEN a political or relational event, WHEN succeeding, THEN the heir inherits it
- GIVEN the predecessor was a parent, WHEN labelling, THEN it is father or mother by sex
- GIVEN a sibling or spouse, WHEN labelling, THEN the right word is used
- GIVEN no known relation, WHEN labelling, THEN it falls back to kin
- GIVEN several relations, WHEN labelling, THEN parent takes precedence

### SuccessionTriggerTests.cs

_Documents what counts as a succession. A succession is not a notice: it DELETES the predecessor's private history from every NPC's memory (SuccessionMemoryPolicy) and marks the rest inherited, for good. Wallace1067, 25/09/2026: switching the active character with The Original Player Switcher was read as a death each way, reframing 24 profiles twice while nobody had died. The states below are the engine's own (ApplyHeirSelectionAction, decompiled)._

- GIVEN another mod switches the player to a living character, WHEN the change fires, THEN it is no succession
- GIVEN the player died and an heir took over, WHEN the change fires, THEN it is a succession by death
- GIVEN the player retired, WHEN the change fires, THEN it is a succession but not a death

### SwayStanceParserTests.cs

_v1.34.2 resilience: sway_opinion moves an NPC's regard for a named THIRD PARTY at the player's word. It used to read its stance as "against = anything that is not the literal word 'for'", so a malformed, blank, or hallucinated stance token silently became the HARMFUL direction: a -6 relation hit, a Rivalry rumour, and a grudge exposure aimed at someone the model never clearly meant to turn the NPC against. This parser is the strict whitelist that replaces that default-to-harm: only a clear "for"/"against" is honoured, everything else is Unrecognized so the verb REFUSES. Get it wrong and a garbled token wounds an innocent third party, or a real "against" is dropped and the persuasion silently does nothing._

- GIVEN the word for, WHEN parsing, THEN it is For
- GIVEN the word against, WHEN parsing, THEN it is Against
- GIVEN mixed case and surrounding whitespace, WHEN parsing, THEN the stance is recognized
- GIVEN a blank or null stance, WHEN parsing, THEN it is Unrecognized
- GIVEN a garbage or near miss token, WHEN parsing, THEN it is Unrecognized

### TaleBandMakeupTests.cs

_What a tale's band is made of. fkasad (Nexus, 07/10/2026), after the Wolf of the Wood: "A party of the lowest ranked bandits available in the game ... 65 looters vs 110 veterans Dawi. I could have auto resolved it at no cost." The band had grown with his men (2.7.3) but stayed looters, the weakest troop in the game. Gabriel (07/10/2026) ratified a "local" band: the bandits of the region, with a share of seasoned men that follows how seasoned the player's own troops are. If this breaks, the climax is a formality again for any veteran company._

- GIVEN a player leading recruits, WHEN the band is made, THEN it is all plain bandits
- GIVEN a player leading veterans, WHEN the band is made, THEN a share is seasoned and the count is kept
- GIVEN bandits with no better troop, WHEN the band is made, THEN every man is that troop
- GIVEN the wolf tale, WHEN loaded, THEN its slavers are the regions bandits

### TaleConceptionPolicyTests.cs

_A tale of intimacy may end on a chance of a child (Gabriel, 10/10/2026). If these rules are wrong, the player is made pregnant by his wife, two women "conceive", the chance a tale names is ignored, or a tale with no chance written gets none at all instead of the game's own._

- GIVEN a man and his wife, WHEN the tale ends, THEN the wife would carry the child
- GIVEN a woman player and her husband, WHEN the tale ends, THEN the player would carry the child
- GIVEN two women or two men, WHEN the tale ends, THEN no child is conceived
- GIVEN a tale that names a chance or none, WHEN read, THEN its own or the games applies
- GIVEN a chance outside zero to one, WHEN checked, THEN it is no chance

### TaleCultureAndGrudgeBannerTests.cs

_Two asks from tashmetu (Nexus, 06/10/2026) on written tales. First: a tale about Battanian woods should be offered by a Battanian lord about a Battanian village, so the giver and the village may each name a culture, one or several._

- GIVEN no culture asked, WHEN any culture is checked, THEN it matches
- GIVEN a tale for battania, WHEN cultures are checked, THEN only battania matches
- GIVEN a tale for several cultures, WHEN checked, THEN any of them matches
- GIVEN a file with one culture or a list, WHEN loaded, THEN both read as lists
- GIVEN a grudge against the player, WHEN the banner is written, THEN it says against you
- GIVEN a grudge between two lords, WHEN the banner is written, THEN it names the target

### TaleElopementPolicyTests.cs

_The Troy-inspired tale (docs/design/tale-scenario-bridge; Gabriel, 12/10/2026): a queen leaves her husband with the player ("elope"), rides with him as a guest who may later be married ("Invitee et epousable"), and her return to her husband is the condition of peace ("Le retour de la Reine a son mari est la condition de paix"). These rules say when each can really happen. If they are wrong, a tale tells the realm she left while the game kept her married, a woman who leads her house is torn from it, a captive "rides away", or she is "returned" to a man already remarried, or after the player married her._

- GIVEN a married lady free to go, WHEN she would elope, THEN she may
- GIVEN what keeps a marriage from ending, WHEN she would elope, THEN she does not
- GIVEN she leads a party or governs, WHEN she would elope, THEN her post is released first
- GIVEN both alive and unwed, WHEN she is returned, THEN the marriage is restored
- GIVEN what closes the way back, WHEN she would be returned, THEN she is not
- GIVEN a husband away on campaign or a captive, WHEN she would be returned, THEN the return waits and is tried again
- GIVEN the way back closed, WHEN decided, THEN it ends and says why
- GIVEN only the games matchmaking rule refusing, WHEN she is returned, THEN the marriage is restored directly

### TaleImageTests.cs

_Images in written tales. Gabriel (08/10/2026): "add images to the quests ... shown in a panel to the left of the chat ... corresponding to stages ... the stories should then be in a folder, to keep the JSON and the images together"; then: in the scenes, opening by themselves, images he provides, and the Tale Editor places them on the stages._

- GIVEN an image named by the givers realm, WHEN its stage opens, THEN the picture of that realm is shown
- GIVEN a role not bound or a value with a path, WHEN resolved, THEN no picture and never outside the folder
- GIVEN an image named by realm, WHEN checked at load, THEN its files are not demanded
- GIVEN a stage with an image, WHEN loaded, THEN it keeps the file name
- GIVEN a scene stage with an image, WHEN its scene opens, THEN its image is the one shown
- GIVEN the village being questioned, WHEN a villager or the giver is spoken to, THEN only the villager conversation is the step
- GIVEN a step to tell the lord, WHEN he or a villager is spoken to, THEN only the lord is the step
- GIVEN a step that is no conversation, WHEN anyone is spoken to, THEN none is the step
- GIVEN choices said to the giver, WHEN she is spoken to, THEN it is the step
- GIVEN a picture, WHEN a character is spoken to, THEN it is small and keeps its proportions
- GIVEN a crvid named as a stage picture, WHEN checked, THEN it is accepted and known for a video
- GIVEN an image present in the tales folder, WHEN checked, THEN no problem
- GIVEN an image missing from the folder, WHEN checked, THEN the author is told
- GIVEN a narration that reveals springs and meets, WHEN the picture is chosen, THEN the person met shows
- GIVEN a finding image missing, WHEN checked, THEN the author is told which
- GIVEN a path or a format not loaded, WHEN checked, THEN it is refused

### TaleSceneFeelTests.cs

_fkasad (Nexus, 07/10/2026), on written tales, ratified by Gabriel ("on fait aussi les idees de fkasad"): - a scene takes the player away from town, so the narration should show where they are: "an Aserai character scouting a forest at night compared to a Battanian doing the same", "a Nord crawling through the sand", "a guy from Danustica lost in the snow"; - "last encounter difficulty should scale with rank/army size. Otherwise, there's no climax scene to end the quest"; - war prisoners can be invited into a tale's scene; that should be the tale's choice, off unless a stage allows it._

- GIVEN an Aserai in a forest on a winter night, WHEN the narrator is told, THEN it knows all of it
- GIVEN the engine names of the ground, WHEN worded, THEN they read as places
- GIVEN nothing known of the surroundings, WHEN composed, THEN nothing is added
- GIVEN the surroundings, WHEN the scene narrator is told, THEN they are in its prompt
- GIVEN a band that does not scale, WHEN sized, THEN it is the files roll
- GIVEN a band that scales, WHEN the player rides alone or with an army, THEN it meets them at their size
- GIVEN a scene that does not welcome prisoners, WHEN the player invites, THEN only their free company is offered
- GIVEN a scene in a tale file, WHEN loaded, THEN prisoners are welcome only when it says so
- GIVEN the wolf tale, WHEN loaded, THEN its slavers scale with the player

### TaleTesterNotesTests.cs

_tashmetu's tester notes on 2.7.3 (Nexus, 07/10/2026), fifteen written tales of his own: - "the interpreter (deepseek-v3.2) twice reported choice_voice for the choice voice, and TakeSaidChoice refused it although the lord had plainly agreed ... The list reaches it as - choice voice: ..., so the model glues the two words"; - "a finished tale stays listed twice, under Old Quests and still under Active Quests"; - "once every required finding is found, the narrator keeps asking What do you do? until the player closes the chat"._

- GIVEN a choice id with the lists word glued on, WHEN matched, THEN it is the open choice
- GIVEN an id that is no open choice, WHEN matched, THEN nothing is taken
- GIVEN a choice by words, WHEN the interpreter is told, THEN its line starts with the id alone
- GIVEN journal entries of tales, WHEN reconciled, THEN only the tale under way keeps one open entry
- GIVEN every required finding found and no way to choose, WHEN the narrator is told, THEN it lets the scene end
- GIVEN a required finding still hidden, WHEN asked, THEN the scene goes on

### TaleUnderWayTests.cs

_fkasad's walk through the Wolf of the Wood on 2.7.3 (Nexus, 07/10/2026), three things ratified by Gabriel: - "I almost clicked on End Conversation instead of Send at least four times during the forest part ... it could be a catastrophe during a quest": closing a scene ends it and moves the tale on, with no way back; - the tale's lord also handed him a hideout task, and the journal showed two targets at once; - "the companions participating ... should have some memory of it. They talked, gave their opinion, advices"._

- GIVEN a scene still going, WHEN the player clicks to end it, THEN they are asked first
- GIVEN a tale under way, WHEN asked who holds a role, THEN its bound characters do and only while it runs
- GIVEN a companion in the scene, WHEN it closes, THEN they remember the search and its findings
- GIVEN many findings, WHEN the memory is written, THEN it stays within bounds

### TeachSkillPolicyTests.cs

_teach_skill closes an empty-promise class the mod fixes everywhere else: NPCs already offer lessons in prose, but nothing granted real skill XP. TeachSkillPolicy is the law the bridge (ExecuteTeachSkill) re-validates against before ever calling HeroDeveloper.AddSkillXp, so these tests are the only protection this silently-breaking rule has. The knobs it pins: a teacher must be genuinely competent (the TeacherSkillFloor gate closes "a novice cannot teach mastery they do not have"), the granted XP is a NUDGE whose ceiling scales with the teacher's own mastery but never exceeds MaxCeiling (never a level-up), and a per-NPC cooldown (mirroring RelationGate's cap+cooldown shape) stops one companion being re-asked for a fresh lesson every turn._

- GIVEN a teacher below the skill floor, WHEN checking CanTeach, THEN it is false
- GIVEN a teacher exactly at the skill floor, WHEN checking CanTeach, THEN it is true
- GIVEN a teacher below the floor, WHEN resolving granted xp, THEN nothing is granted
- GIVEN a teacher just at the floor, WHEN a generous amount is requested, THEN it is capped to the minimum ceiling
- GIVEN a master teacher at the skill cap, WHEN a huge amount is requested, THEN it is capped to the maximum ceiling
- GIVEN a mid tier teacher, WHEN a generous amount is requested, THEN the ceiling sits between the floor and the cap
- GIVEN a requested amount below the ceiling, WHEN resolving granted xp, THEN the requested amount lands unclamped
- GIVEN a second lesson within the cooldown window, WHEN resolving granted xp, THEN nothing is granted
- GIVEN a lesson after the cooldown window has elapsed, WHEN resolving granted xp, THEN it lands again
- GIVEN the teacher has never taught before, WHEN resolving granted xp, THEN it is not blocked by the cooldown
- GIVEN a non positive requested amount, WHEN resolving granted xp, THEN nothing is granted
- GIVEN a master and a player who knows less, WHEN asked, THEN the lesson can be given
- GIVEN a player who already knows more than the master, WHEN asked, THEN there is nothing to teach
- GIVEN a teacher and player at the same level, WHEN asked, THEN it is not a lesson
- GIVEN the same two people the other way round, WHEN asked, THEN the answer inverts
- GIVEN a teacher below the floor who still knows more than the player, WHEN asked, THEN no lesson

### ThirdPartyDescriptorTests.cs

_Protects how an NPC describes (and locates) third parties the player names — relation thresholds and the kin/clan/faction gating of gossip. These run on every name-bearing message and break in silence: a drift would have lords mislabel friends as strangers, or omnisciently place everyone._

- GIVEN a spouse, WHEN categorizing, THEN kinship wins over any relation score
- GIVEN a parent or child, WHEN categorizing, THEN each kinship is named
- GIVEN deep regard, WHEN categorizing, THEN they are a very close friend
- GIVEN warmth at the friend threshold, WHEN categorizing, THEN they are a friend
- GIVEN deep hostility, WHEN categorizing, THEN they are a bitter enemy
- GIVEN mild hostility at the enemy threshold, WHEN categorizing, THEN they are an enemy
- GIVEN a neutral clanmate, WHEN categorizing, THEN clan membership is named
- GIVEN two same turf gang leaders at neutral standing, WHEN categorizing, THEN they are named rivals not strangers
- GIVEN rival gang leaders who happen to hold regard, WHEN categorizing, THEN the rivalry overrides the friendly label
- GIVEN rival gang leaders who are genuine enemies, WHEN categorizing, THEN the stronger enemy label is kept
- GIVEN a faint acquaintance outside any clan, WHEN categorizing, THEN they are an acquaintance
- GIVEN a stranger, WHEN categorizing, THEN they are not personally known
- GIVEN relation scores across the range, WHEN describing, THEN each tier is named
- GIVEN two people in the same settlement, WHEN judging knowledge, THEN it is certain
- GIVEN close kin or clan, WHEN judging knowledge, THEN it is confident regardless of relation
- GIVEN only a friendly bond, WHEN judging knowledge, THEN it is recent hearsay
- GIVEN no personal tie but a shared faction, WHEN judging knowledge, THEN only rough movements are known
- GIVEN a distant stranger, WHEN judging knowledge, THEN nothing is known
- GIVEN no knowledge, WHEN building a whereabouts clause, THEN there is none
- GIVEN present together, WHEN building a whereabouts clause, THEN it names the shared place
- GIVEN a known person whose place is unreadable, WHEN building a clause, THEN honest uncertainty is voiced
- GIVEN each knowing tier, WHEN building a clause, THEN the framing matches the tie
- GIVEN a woman, WHEN naming her gender, THEN it reads a woman
- GIVEN a man, WHEN naming his gender, THEN it reads a man
- GIVEN this npc holds the prisoner, WHEN clausing, THEN it is present tense and forbids a false backstory
- GIVEN the player holds the prisoner, WHEN clausing, THEN it names the player
- GIVEN a third party holds the prisoner, WHEN clausing, THEN it names the captor
- GIVEN a third party holds the prisoner but the captor is unknown, WHEN clausing, THEN it says someone else
- GIVEN the person is free, WHEN clausing, THEN there is no clause

### TownFoodPolicyTests.cs

_A hungry town remembers who fed it, and who emptied its market (fkasad, Nexus, 01/10/2026: "a large quantity of grain sold at a low price (such as after a siege). That is the sort of thing a starving population would remember. And, of course, the reverse applies, such as buying up all the supplies in a city's market."). If these rules are wrong, a town remembers every sack of grain a trader passes through, or a famine relief goes unnoticed, or a player is branded a hoarder for buying his own rations in a well-fed town._

- GIVEN a town starving or low and falling, WHEN read, THEN it is hungry and a town merely low but rising is not
- GIVEN a hungry town, WHEN the player sells it a large load of food, THEN it is remembered as relief
- GIVEN a hungry town, WHEN the player buys up its food, THEN it is remembered as hoarding
- GIVEN a small trade or a well fed town, WHEN judged, THEN nothing is remembered
- GIVEN food both sold and bought, WHEN judged, THEN the balance decides
- GIVEN a character whose town is hungry, WHEN the grain he asked for arrives, THEN it goes to the closest hungry place
- GIVEN no hungry place, WHEN the grain arrives, THEN it stays with the character who asked
- GIVEN a delivery, WHEN it fills the stores, THEN they rise by the load up to the ceiling
- GIVEN a memory recorded days ago, WHEN judged again, THEN nothing new until the week has passed

### TownNeedsPolicyTests.cs

_fkasad (Nexus, 01/10/2026): "a 'bring me' iron ore, horses, beer, hardwood, whatever the city needs", read from what the town's own market shows. A trade good that costs twice its worth in a town is one it is short of. If these rules are wrong, a governor asks for goods his market is full of, or never asks for what it truly lacks, or a supply for his town and a supply for his own host are confused at the hand-over._

- GIVEN a trade good twice its worth or more, WHEN read, THEN the town lacks it
- GIVEN something that is not a trade good, WHEN read, THEN it is never a need
- GIVEN several goods, WHEN the most lacking is asked, THEN it is the dearest of those lacking
- GIVEN a market good, WHEN keyed, THEN its category reads back and the personal keys are not market goods

### TraitBandTests.cs

_Pins how a Bannerlord trait level (the small signed integer hero.GetTraitLevel returns) becomes the plain band word "low"/"moderate"/"high"._

- GIVEN a negative level, WHEN describing, THEN it reads low
- GIVEN a zero level, WHEN describing, THEN it reads moderate
- GIVEN a positive level, WHEN describing, THEN it reads high

### TransientPartyLedgerTests.cs

_Documents the bookkeeping behind the mod's transient parties (couriers, sergeants, bounty hunters), the one service that replaces the same shape copied three times (the event-listeners map's proposal, 26/09/2026). The stakes: a party forgotten by the ledger is a party a save would carry (a mod party in the save file), and a party remembered after it is gone makes its owner steer a ghost._

- GIVEN parties of several roles, WHEN a save asks, THEN every one is listed
- GIVEN tracked parties, WHEN an owner looks by role or tag, THEN it finds only its own
- GIVEN a party the mod never tracked, WHEN looked up, THEN it is not ours
- GIVEN a party tracked twice, WHEN counted, THEN it is one entry with the latest role
- GIVEN parties gone from the world, WHEN swept, THEN they are forgotten
- GIVEN a blank id or role, WHEN tracked, THEN nothing is recorded

### TravellingAudiencePolicyTests.cs

_Who travelling with the player may ask for a private word, and who asks next. HakiTakiUmba (Nexus, 09/10/2026): his family riding in his party (siblings, a spouse) never asked for a word, only the tavern companions did. Gabriel (09/10/2026): the spouse already has her own channels, so balance it. If this breaks, family stays mute on the road, or one voice (a wife with a long shared past, say) takes every audience and the companions fall silent._

- GIVEN family riding in the players party, WHEN the roster is drawn, THEN they may ask for a word
- GIVEN a hired companion in the party, WHEN the roster is drawn, THEN they ask as a companion
- GIVEN family away or a stranger in the party, WHEN the roster is drawn, THEN they do not ask
- GIVEN several with something to say, WHEN choosing, THEN the strongest matter is heard
- GIVEN the last audience was the wifes, WHEN a companion also has a matter, THEN the companion speaks next
- GIVEN only the last speaker has a matter, WHEN choosing, THEN they still ask
- GIVEN equal matters, WHEN choosing, THEN the first listed asks

### TruncationFallbackPolicyTests.cs

_Documents the PROSE FALLBACK decision (Prose + Interpreter composition): whether a beat the prose model just produced should be redone, in one INTEGRATED call, on the configured fallback (grok) model. The prose call is issued with the SDK's internal double-budget retry disabled so a truncated reply returns immediately; this is what decides, from that reply's finish reason alone, whether the fast failure earns the fallback._

- GIVEN a length finish reason, WHEN a fallback model is configured, THEN it falls back
- GIVEN a content filter finish reason, WHEN a fallback model is configured, THEN it falls back
- GIVEN a null finish reason, WHEN a fallback model is configured, THEN it falls back
- GIVEN a stop finish reason, WHEN a fallback model is configured, THEN it does not fall back
- GIVEN a clean stop but no spoken dialogue, WHEN a fallback model is configured, THEN it falls back
- GIVEN no spoken dialogue, WHEN no fallback model is configured, THEN it does not fall back
- GIVEN a clean stop with a spoken line, WHEN a fallback model is configured, THEN it does not fall back
- GIVEN a truncated finish reason, WHEN no fallback model is configured, THEN it does not fall back

### TrystPolicyTests.cs

_Guards TrystPolicy (Inc 3b-a): whether a partner who has taken a lover MEETS him this week. A tryst may happen only when she has a lover, the player is not beside her (an unobserved meeting), and a modest weekly roll lands. This is the hook the wife-to-lover bond growth, the secret-affair discovery curve, and the later pregnancy-by-the-lover build on. Get it wrong and trysts fire while the player rides at her side (she meets her lover under the husband's nose, which the design forbids), or never fire at all (the whole 3b/3c line is dead), or fire for a partner with no lover (a tryst with no one)._

- GIVEN a partner with no lover, WHEN asked, THEN no tryst
- GIVEN the player is beside her, WHEN asked, THEN no tryst
- GIVEN a lover and the player away, WHEN the roll lands under the chance, THEN they meet

### UndeliverableReasonDescriberTests.cs

_Documents the failure-report wording: which cause an undeliverable letter resolves to, and the exact line the player reads. Before this batch every failure said "could not be reached", so the player never learned that the recipient DIED or was CAPTURED mid-flight (the courier robustness batch, ROADMAP 2026-07-20). The wording is load-bearing: it is the only channel through which the player learns the fate of a letter's recipient._

- GIVEN a dead recipient, WHEN the failure is described, THEN the player learns of the death
- GIVEN a captured recipient, WHEN the failure is described, THEN the player learns of the captivity
- GIVEN a recipient both dead and flagged prisoner, WHEN resolved, THEN death wins
- GIVEN a vanished recipient, WHEN the failure is described, THEN the wording stays generic

### UnknownActionNoticeTests.cs

_Pins the wording and the diagnostic block for an [ACTION] the bridge refused because its type is not a real mod verb. This is the feedback funnel: the player sees an immersive, deed-scoped line (never "nothing happened", which contradicted a triumphant scene where a +1 regard and a quest DID land that turn), and the invented verb is always logged with its context so the author can add it later. What each channel says is what these tests hold in place._

- GIVEN the base message, WHEN read, THEN it is deed scoped and never says nothing happened
- GIVEN reporting is off, WHEN composing, THEN only the base message shows
- GIVEN reporting is on, WHEN composing, THEN the base message carries a person free report hint
- GIVEN a full context, WHEN formatting the log entry, THEN it captures the verb params npc and lines
- GIVEN nulls and blanks, WHEN formatting the log entry, THEN blank fields are omitted and it never throws

### VassalageOfferPolicyTests.cs

_join_as_vassal fix (player report, 2026-08-05): "asked the legitimate ruler to become her vassal, the LLM narrated acceptance and threw 6000 denars, but the game only ever registered the player as a mercenary." Verifies the two guards the bridge consults before swearing the player's clan into vassalage, so a narrated oath must actually change the player's kingdom standing from mercenary/independent to full vassal, or be surfaced as refused - never a silent no-op that leaves the fiction and the save diverged._

- GIVEN the npc is the kingdom ruler and the player is not already a vassal, WHEN resolving join as vassal, THEN the oath is allowed
- GIVEN the npc is not the kingdom ruler, WHEN resolving join as vassal, THEN the oath is refused
- GIVEN the player is already a full vassal, WHEN resolving join as vassal, THEN the oath is refused
- GIVEN the player is at war with the kingdom, WHEN resolving join as vassal, THEN the oath is refused
- GIVEN the npc is not the ruler and the player is already a vassal, WHEN resolving join as vassal, THEN the oath is refused

### VentriloquizedReactionPolicyTests.cs

_Round-table audit D7. When the player opens the floor, the lap that follows gives each person in the room a real turn in their own voice. The main NPC's [WITNESS_REACTION] lines on that same turn are the model ventriloquizing those same people, so showing them made a witness speak twice in a row, and since the two lines come from two different model calls the second could flatly contradict the first. This policy picks which voice wins. It was an inline condition in ChatViewModel.Exchanges and therefore untestable (the mod is net472, this project is net10)._

- GIVEN the floor is open on the hosts turn, WHEN deciding, THEN the reactions are dropped
- GIVEN no floor request is pending, WHEN deciding, THEN the reactions are kept
- GIVEN a witness and not the host is replying, WHEN deciding, THEN the reactions are kept
- GIVEN a captive scene, WHEN deciding, THEN the reactions are kept
- GIVEN the conversation has already ended, WHEN deciding, THEN the reactions are kept

### VerbRegistryCoreTests.cs

_The pure dispatch/composition engine behind CalradiaRemembers.Verbs.VerbRegistry, which registers BOTH the mod's own conversation verbs and any ExternalVerbAdapter wrapping a third-party mod's ExternalVerbContract in that very same list. So the crash-containment and lookup rules pinned here are what stands between one faulty external verb and every player's conversation window: the v1.21.0 regression (a verb's IsEligible touching a missing game API closed EVERY chat) is exactly the failure mode a hostile or outdated third-party verb could reproduce, by accident or otherwise. Case-insensitive name routing, the null-vs- nothing-composed sentinel, and explicit-order composition are the mod's own dispatch guarantees this class has to keep honest for external verbs too, not just its own._

- GIVEN one eligible and one ineligible verb, WHEN composing teachings, THEN only the eligible one is included
- GIVEN verbs of two kinds, WHEN composing only one kind, THEN its eligible verbs are taught and no other
- GIVEN multiple eligible verbs, WHEN composing without an explicit order, THEN they concatenate in registration order
- GIVEN no eligible verbs, WHEN composing teachings, THEN the result is null
- GIVEN an eligible verb whose teaching text is empty, WHEN composing, THEN nothing is appended for it
- GIVEN an explicit order naming a subset, WHEN composing teachings, THEN only those names are composed in that order
- GIVEN an explicit order naming an unregistered verb, WHEN composing teachings, THEN that name is skipped
- GIVEN an ineligible verb that states its door, WHEN composing, THEN it speaks though its teaching is hidden
- GIVEN a verb that chooses silence, WHEN composing, THEN nothing is contributed
- GIVEN a verb not implementing IStatesUnavailability, WHEN composing, THEN it contributes nothing
- GIVEN a verb that throws stating its door, WHEN composing, THEN it is skipped and the others still speak
- GIVEN several verbs stating doors, WHEN composing, THEN they come back in registration order
- GIVEN an ineligible verb with guidance text, WHEN composing guidance, THEN it is included though composing teachings excludes it
- GIVEN a verb not implementing IProvidesGuidance, WHEN composing guidance, THEN it contributes nothing
- GIVEN a guidance callback that throws, WHEN composing guidance, THEN it is skipped and others still compose
- GIVEN no verb offers guidance text, WHEN composing guidance, THEN the result is null
- GIVEN a verb whose eligibility throws, WHEN composing teachings, THEN it is skipped and the others still compose
- GIVEN a faulting verb and a fault sink, WHEN composing teachings, THEN the exception is reported to the sink
- GIVEN an action name matching a registered verb, WHEN executing, THEN that verbs Execute runs and its result is returned
- GIVEN an action name with no matching verb, WHEN executing, THEN the result is null
- GIVEN an action name differing only by case, WHEN executing, THEN it still routes to the matching verb
- GIVEN a registered verb, WHEN unregistered by name, THEN it no longer executes or teaches
- GIVEN a registered verb, WHEN unregistered with a differently cased name, THEN it still removes it
- GIVEN an unknown or blank name, WHEN Unregister is called, THEN it returns false without throwing

### WarDeedMatcherTests.cs

_Guards the declare-war deed: it completes only when the PLAYER declared war on the demanded faction — never when the player was attacked, and never on an unrelated war. A drift would credit (or deny) the deed for the wrong war._

- GIVEN the player declares war on the demanded faction, WHEN judging, THEN it is satisfied
- GIVEN war was declared ON the player, WHEN judging, THEN it is NOT satisfied
- GIVEN war with a different faction, WHEN judging, THEN it is NOT satisfied
- GIVEN case differences, WHEN judging, THEN matching is case insensitive
- GIVEN missing ids, WHEN judging, THEN it is NOT satisfied

### WarDutySummonsTests.cs

_Whether and how the realm's ruler calls in a vassal he holds to the war duty (Gabriel's ladder, 15/10/2026: the vassal is summoned to come and answer for sitting out the war). Until now the summons came only by letter, and the ruler sat in his hall: a vassal who walked into his town was never called in, and a ruler's field party kept him out of the fief audience altogether. Wrong one way, the summoned vassal stands beside his liege and nothing is said, or another lord's errand takes the meeting; wrong the other way, a ruler who already heard the vassal keeps sending for him, or a chase that missed writes a second letter beside the duty's own._

- GIVEN a ruler holding the reckoning and a grudge, WHEN the reason is chosen, THEN he calls the vassal in over the war
- GIVEN a ruler holding the reckoning on cooldown, WHEN the reason is chosen, THEN he does not come
- GIVEN the war duty summons, WHEN asked whether it sends the ruler out on the road, THEN it never does
- GIVEN the war duty summons, WHEN narrowed to a fief, THEN it is kept
- GIVEN a notable and the summoning ruler in the same town, WHEN one is chosen, THEN the ruler calls the vassal in
- GIVEN a ruler with a field party in this town, WHEN asked whether he may seek an audience, THEN only the summoning ruler may
- GIVEN a war duty chase that came to nothing, WHEN the letter is judged, THEN no missed meeting letter is written
- GIVEN each step of the reckoning, WHEN asked whether the ruler calls the vassal in, THEN only before he has come

### WarDutyTests.cs

_What the vassal war duty decides: whether the player owes his liege service in a war (WarDutyPolicy), the reckoning that follows when he gave none (WarDutyLadder), and the levy in coin that stands for his absence (WarLevyPolicy)._

- GIVEN a sworn vassal who sat out a month of war, WHEN judged, THEN he owes service
- GIVEN a mercenary, WHEN judged, THEN he owes no fealty
- GIVEN the player rules the realm, WHEN judged, THEN no one calls him to account
- GIVEN a captive vassal, WHEN judged, THEN he owes nothing while held
- GIVEN a realm at peace, WHEN judged, THEN no duty is owed
- GIVEN a war younger than the window, WHEN judged, THEN no one is called yet
- GIVEN a vassal sworn a week ago, WHEN judged, THEN he is not held to account yet
- GIVEN a vassal who fought within the window, WHEN judged, THEN he has served
- GIVEN a reckoning already opened this war, WHEN judged again, THEN he is not summoned twice
- GIVEN a window set absurdly low, WHEN judged, THEN the window is held to its floor
- GIVEN a reckoning opened, WHEN it starts, THEN the player is summoned with time to come
- GIVEN a summoned vassal, WHEN he comes before the ruler, THEN the levy is demanded in person
- GIVEN a summons ignored past its deadline, WHEN the day passes, THEN the levy is demanded by letter
- GIVEN the deadline day itself, WHEN the day passes, THEN nothing happens yet
- GIVEN a vassal owing the levy, WHEN he serves the realm, THEN the reckoning is settled
- GIVEN the levy paid partly in coin and partly in horses, WHEN the total meets it, THEN it is settled
- GIVEN nothing served or paid by the last deadline, WHEN the player holds a fief, THEN a fief is taken
- GIVEN nothing served or paid, WHEN the player holds no fief, THEN the ruler remembers the default
- GIVEN peace before the levy is paid, WHEN the duty ends, THEN the reckoning is dropped without a loss
- GIVEN a settled reckoning, WHEN its old deadline passes, THEN nothing is taken
- GIVEN a greater house with fiefs, WHEN the levy is set, THEN it asks more than of a minor clan
- GIVEN a vast house and a high scale, WHEN the levy is set, THEN it never exceeds one transfer
- GIVEN a summons, WHEN its context is worded, THEN it names the war the days and the levy that follows
- GIVEN a landless vassal, WHEN the levy letter is worded, THEN no fief is threatened
- GIVEN a vassal with a fief, WHEN the levy letter is worded, THEN the fief goods and service are named
- GIVEN a town and a castle, WHEN a fief is confiscated, THEN the castle is taken
- GIVEN two castles, WHEN a fief is confiscated, THEN the poorer is taken
- GIVEN his only fief under siege, WHEN judged, THEN nothing can be taken
- GIVEN a scale of zero, WHEN the levy is set, THEN the levy as designed is asked

### WarLevyPaymentTests.cs

_What a hand-over counts for against the war levy, and what the liege is told of the terms in person. Gabriel (15/10/2026): a vassal who will not pay the levy in coin "pourrait fournir des troupes, des chevaux, des armes", and the liege holds him to account face to face. Wrong one way, a levy can never be met in goods or men, or the liege names a way to pay the game will refuse; wrong the other way, a cart of cloth settles a war levy, or paying it strips the player's party bare._

- GIVEN each kind of goods, WHEN handed to the liege, THEN only horses and weapons count toward the levy
- GIVEN goods worth more than is owed, WHEN counted, THEN they count for what is owed
- GIVEN recruits and veterans, WHEN soldiers pay the levy, THEN the cheapest go first until it is met
- GIVEN a levy worth the whole party, WHEN soldiers pay it, THEN no more than half the men are taken
- GIVEN a liege whose party is nearly full, WHEN soldiers pay, THEN no more are taken than it can hold
- GIVEN a levy partly paid, WHEN the terms are worded, THEN the liege knows what is still owed and what follows
- GIVEN a liege who cannot take soldiers now, WHEN the terms are worded, THEN soldiers are not offered
- GIVEN nothing owed, WHEN the terms are worded, THEN there are none

### WarLoomingPolicyTests.cs

_Guards WarLoomingPolicy, the gate deciding whether a pair of kingdoms not yet at war is tense enough to record a WarLooming WorldEvent (which then spreads as rumour, "what you've heard", and the chronicle). Its answer feeds the weekly world-conditions scan directly: a wrong "yes" spams the realm with phantom war scares, a wrong "no" leaves the world silent about a war everyone in Calradia would be bracing for. Each boundary below pins one of those failures. Rationale derived from the policy's own header/docs._

- GIVEN the pair is already at war, WHEN deciding, THEN it is not recorded
- GIVEN peace is fresher than the minimum, WHEN deciding, THEN it is not recorded
- GIVEN peace exactly at the minimum age, WHEN all else holds, THEN it is recorded
- GIVEN the war score is below threshold, WHEN deciding, THEN it is not recorded
- GIVEN the war score exactly at threshold, WHEN all else holds, THEN it is recorded
- GIVEN the same pair loomed too recently, WHEN deciding, THEN it is not recorded
- GIVEN the cooldown has exactly elapsed, WHEN all else holds, THEN it is recorded
- GIVEN the pair has never loomed before, WHEN all else holds, THEN it is recorded

### WarServiceLedgerTests.cs

_What WarServiceLedger remembers for the vassal war duty (Maifailun, Gabriel, 15/10/2026): since when the player has been sworn to his realm, since when the realm has been at war, when he last served it, and whether this stretch of war already had its reckoning. The game keeps none of it. If it is wrong, a vassal is summoned the day he loads an old save, the day he swears, twice in one war, or never at all._

- GIVEN an old save loaded mid war, WHEN the first day is judged, THEN no one owes yet
- GIVEN a month of war without service, WHEN judged, THEN he owes
- GIVEN a battle fought for the realm last week, WHEN judged, THEN he has served
- GIVEN a vassal who swore to a new realm, WHEN judged, THEN his count starts again
- GIVEN a reckoning opened in this war, WHEN judged later in it, THEN he is not summoned again
- GIVEN peace then a new war, WHEN the new war runs a month unserved, THEN he owes again
- GIVEN a ledger with a reckoning, WHEN saved and loaded, THEN it reads the same
- GIVEN a corrupt saved ledger, WHEN loaded, THEN an empty ledger is read

### WarWearinessTests.cs

_Documents companion war-weariness: wounds and captures accumulate a toll, rest eases it, Valor sets how much a companion bears before wanting out, and the retirement they ask for depends on whether they hold land._

- GIVEN a battle wound, WHEN applied, THEN the toll rises by one
- GIVEN a capture, WHEN applied, THEN the toll rises by ten
- GIVEN a score near the ceiling, WHEN more toll lands, THEN it clamps to max
- GIVEN rest, WHEN easing the toll, THEN it falls by the step without going below zero
- GIVEN a non positive rest step, WHEN resting, THEN the toll is unchanged
- GIVEN a neutral valor, WHEN reading the tolerance, THEN it is the base
- GIVEN a braver companion, WHEN reading the tolerance, THEN they bear more war
- GIVEN an extreme valor, WHEN reading the tolerance, THEN it stays within sane bounds
- GIVEN the toll reaches the tolerance, WHEN checking, THEN the companion wants out
- GIVEN the same toll, WHEN one is braver, THEN only the cautious one wants out
- GIVEN rising toll, WHEN banding a neutral companion, THEN it climbs fresh to spent
- GIVEN the same toll, WHEN valor differs, THEN the band shifts with tolerance
- GIVEN a landed companion, WHEN they retire, THEN they step back to govern
- GIVEN a landless companion, WHEN they retire, THEN they leave your service
- GIVEN each band, WHEN describing, THEN the reading reflects the band
- GIVEN the minimum service days, WHEN reading the constant, THEN it is two calradian years
- GIVEN service at exactly the minimum, WHEN checking tenure, THEN it has been served long enough
- GIVEN service one day short of the minimum, WHEN checking tenure, THEN it has not been served long enough
- GIVEN a brand new recruit, WHEN checking tenure, THEN it has not been served long enough

### WeddingMemoryTextTests.cs

_Documents the memory a new spouse keeps of the wedding. zhenja1, 25/09/2026: after a vanilla marriage offer, his bride spoke at their first word as though she had always been part of his house. Nothing had been written on her at the wedding, and an empty history makes the prompt say "you are already married and share a life". What this line says is what the model will build her whole manner on, so it must say how new the marriage is, and never less._

- GIVEN an arranged match with someone never met, WHEN she remembers the wedding, THEN she knows they are still strangers
- GIVEN an arranged match with someone met once, WHEN she remembers the wedding, THEN she barely knows him
- GIVEN a couple who courted in conversation, WHEN she remembers the wedding, THEN it is simply the wedding
- GIVEN a spouse with no house of her own, WHEN the match is described, THEN no houses are named

### WifeTaleKhuzaitTests.cs

_"Beneath the Falls", the Khuzait wife's tale, as it ships in the Intimate Tales module (Gabriel, 10/10/2026: the player finds his wife bathing, she joins him, he admires her in states he may pass between and back, she lies down and invites him; the ending strengthens their bond and may bring a child). If these tests fail, the file no longer loads, a state leads nowhere or cannot be left, a picture is missing, or the ending forgets her memory or the child._

- GIVEN the shipped tale, WHEN read, THEN it asks for his khuzait wife with him on the map
- GIVEN the shipped tale, WHEN its pictures are checked, THEN each stage after the invitation has one and it is there
- GIVEN the states, WHEN their ways are read, THEN each leads to every other and to the invitation
- GIVEN a walk through the tale, WHEN the words move it, THEN she speaks from each state and it ends rewarded
- GIVEN the ending, WHEN read, THEN it leaves her a memory regard and a chance of a child

### WifeTalesTests.cs

_The seven wife tales of the Intimate Tales module, one per people (Gabriel, 11/10/2026: "Complete-les tous et demain je pourrai verifier tout ca et tester"). Each follows the shape of Beneath the Falls: the player finds his wife bathing (sometimes watching a while, she knowing it), she joins him, he admires her in states he may pass between and back, she invites him (sometimes through further moments, sometimes an afterwards), and the ending leaves her a memory, regard and a chance of a child. If these tests fail, a tale no longer loads, asks for the wrong wife, misses a picture, strands the player in a state, or cannot be walked to its ending with her speaking from each step._

- GIVEN the seven tales, WHEN loaded together, THEN all seven load without a problem
- GIVEN a wife tale, WHEN read, THEN it asks for his wife of that people on the map
- GIVEN a wife tale, WHEN its pictures are checked, THEN every stage has one and it is there
- GIVEN a wife tale, WHEN its ways are read, THEN every one is taken by words with her
- GIVEN a wife tale, WHEN walked to its ending, THEN she speaks from each stage and it ends rewarded

### WitnessDedupTests.cs

_Documents when two witness entries are the same person, so the "Present:" list never shows a duplicate (player report: bringing a companion who is already present listed their name twice)._

- GIVEN two entries with the same hero id, WHEN compared, THEN they are the same even if names differ
- GIVEN two entries with different hero ids, WHEN compared, THEN they are distinct
- GIVEN a hero and a same named heroless bystander, WHEN compared, THEN they stay distinct
- GIVEN two heroless entries with the same name, WHEN compared, THEN they are the same
- GIVEN two heroless entries with different names, WHEN compared, THEN they are distinct
- GIVEN a null entry, WHEN compared, THEN it is never the same

### WitnessPresencePolicyTests.cs

_This policy decides how many AMBIENT hero witnesses a gathered conversation actually keeps, once the resolver has already collected them (WitnessResolver.ResolveAutoWitnesses / ApplyWitnessDensity). Its answer feeds straight into the system prompt: every witness it keeps gets a voice, a portrait, and a slot in [WITNESS_REACTION] exchanges, so an over-generous keep means the same crowded room players complained about, and an over-eager thinning means a lord's own audience quietly loses its retinue for no reason the player can see. The mod is LIVE, so the single rule every test here ultimately serves is: Bustling, the shipped default, must change NOTHING for a player who never opens the menu. The rng is injected so the crowd stays unpredictable in play (backed by MBRandom in the engine) while remaining fully deterministic here._

- GIVEN Bustling, WHEN filtering a large room, THEN every witness is kept unchanged
- GIVEN Bustling, WHEN the rng returns just under one, THEN nothing is dropped
- GIVEN Sparse, WHEN every witness would pass the probability roll, THEN the room is still capped to one
- GIVEN Normal, WHEN every witness would pass the probability roll, THEN the room is capped to two
- GIVEN Normal, WHEN the rng returns just below the probability, THEN the witness is kept
- GIVEN Normal, WHEN the rng returns exactly the probability, THEN the witness is dropped
- GIVEN Sparse, WHEN the rng straddles its own cutoff, THEN below keeps and at or above drops
- GIVEN an empty eligible list, WHEN filtering at any density, THEN the result is empty
- GIVEN a null eligible list, WHEN filtering, THEN the result is an empty list not a crash
- GIVEN a room smaller than the cap, WHEN filtering, THEN the whole room survives
- GIVEN several witnesses survive the roll, WHEN capped, THEN their original order is preserved
- GIVEN a null rng, WHEN filtering, THEN it degrades to an empty room instead of throwing
- GIVEN the three density levels, WHEN their probability and cap are read, THEN they match the shipped mapping
- GIVEN the three levels, WHEN ordered, THEN each one up genuinely means a fuller room

### WitnessReactionGateTests.cs

_This gate decides which [WITNESS_REACTION] lines a model emitted may be VOICED, and the stake is larger than a stray bubble on screen: a kept line is committed to the transcript, so it is replayed into every prompt rebuilt afterwards. A wrongly-kept line therefore does not appear once, it joins the conversation's history and the model keeps that speaker talking. The rule lived inside ChatViewModel until now, which meant it had NO test at all (the mod is net472, this project is net10), and that is exactly how one of these faults shipped and reached players through the 15C witness path._

- GIVEN a line from someone in the room, WHEN deciding, THEN it is voiced
- GIVEN a line from someone who is not in the room, WHEN deciding, THEN it is dropped
- GIVEN a line attributed to the speaker themselves, WHEN deciding, THEN it is dropped
- GIVEN the same line this speaker just said, WHEN deciding, THEN it is dropped
- GIVEN a repeat written in different casing, WHEN deciding, THEN it is still caught
- GIVEN a different line from the same speaker, WHEN deciding, THEN it is voiced
- GIVEN a name carrying its prompt role, WHEN deciding, THEN it is matched and named canonically
- GIVEN a synthetic witness with no hero behind it, WHEN deciding, THEN it may still speak
- GIVEN an empty line, WHEN deciding, THEN it is dropped as blank
- GIVEN nobody present, WHEN deciding, THEN nothing may be voiced

### WitnessSpeechDetectorTests.cs

_Player report: a non-active witness gives a wordless gesture ("*smirk deepens, remains silent, fingers drumming*") and the active NPC's Sprint 15C auto-reply then treats it as being blown off, escalating against a silence the same model just invented. This classifier is what the 15C gate consults to decide whether a witness reaction actually SPOKE; a wrong answer here either lets a pure gesture keep driving the auto-reply loop (the bug persists) or silences a witness who genuinely spoke (a real reply vanishes)._

- GIVEN the reported bug string, WHEN checking, THEN it has no spoken words
- GIVEN a gesture only reaction, WHEN checking, THEN it has no spoken words
- GIVEN a quoted line, WHEN checking, THEN it has spoken words
- GIVEN a gesture plus a quoted line, WHEN checking, THEN it has spoken words
- GIVEN plain prose with no asterisks, WHEN checking, THEN it has spoken words
- GIVEN null or whitespace, WHEN checking, THEN it has no spoken words
- GIVEN multiple gesture spans and nothing else, WHEN checking, THEN it has no spoken words
- GIVEN a stray unmatched asterisk around real words, WHEN checking, THEN it has spoken words

### WorldEventPrunerTests.cs

_Keeps the persisted world-event log bounded: WorldEventStore serializes the whole list to a save-file byte[] on every save, so an unbounded log is a growth-without-limit risk to the save (the same class of failure the mod already guards against for the NPC profile store).
